`apps/server/app/status/forgetUser.test.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { InMemoryReadModelStore, foldedStates } from "@loom/plugin-read-model";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CHAT_ACTIVITY_MODEL, ChatActivity } from "../chats/activity";
import { chatConversationId } from "../chats/ids";
import { InMemoryProgressBus } from "../documents/progress";
import { type UserExecutionLister, forgetUserRows } from "./forgetUser";
import { IngestStatus } from "./ingestStatus";
import { INGEST_STATUS_MODEL } from "./model";
import { MemoryLog, evt, ingestRun } from "./testing";

const [A, B] = [randomUUID(), randomUUID()];
const [docA, docB, chatA, chatB] = [randomUUID(), randomUUID(), randomUUID(), randomUUID()];
const ingestOf = (u: string, d: string) => `u_${u}:ingest-${d}`;
const titleOf = (u: string, c: string) => `u_${u}:chat-title-${c}`;

/** What the Loom-pool lister does, over the log the models folded: the user's executions by id prefix. */
const listFrom =
  (log: MemoryLog): UserExecutionLister =>
  async (_model, userId) =>
    (await log.listExecutions()).filter(
      (id) => id === `u_${userId}` || id.startsWith(`u_${userId}:`) || id.startsWith(`u_${userId}.c-`),
    );

function setup() {
  const store = new InMemoryReadModelStore();
  const log = new MemoryLog();
  const userExecutions = listFrom(log);
  const status = new IngestStatus({
    writer: store,
    readerFor: () => store,
    source: log,
    sensitive: {},
    bus: new InMemoryProgressBus(),
    userExecutions,
    onError: () => {},
  });
  const activity = new ChatActivity({
    writer: store,
    source: log,
    sensitive: {},
    userExecutions,
    onError: () => {},
  });
  const conv = (u: string, c: string) => chatConversationId(u, c);
  log.append(
    ...ingestRun(ingestOf(A, docA), "ready"),
    ...ingestRun(ingestOf(B, docB), "ready"),
    ...ingestRun(titleOf(A, chatA), "started"), // a title execution: a NULL-scope row in both models
    evt(conv(A, chatA), 0, "EXECUTION_STARTED", { input: {} }), // a chat: a NULL-scope row in ingest-status
    evt(conv(B, chatB), 0, "EXECUTION_STARTED", { input: {} }),
  );
  const rows = async (model: string) =>
    (
      await store.getFolded(model, [
        ingestOf(A, docA),
        ingestOf(B, docB),
        titleOf(A, chatA),
        conv(A, chatA),
        conv(B, chatB),
      ])
    ).map((r) => ({ id: r.executionId, state: r.state, erased: r.erased }));
  return { store, log, status, activity, rows, conv };
}

describe("forgetUser over both models", () => {
  it("tombstones the user's rows in each model, the NULL-scope ones included, and only that user's", async () => {
    const { status, activity, rows, conv } = setup();
    await status.boot();
    await activity.boot();

    // ingest-status folds EVERY execution: A has three rows (ingest, title, chat), B two.
    expect(await status.forgetUser(A)).toBe(3);
    // chat-activity: A's chat row and the neutral rows of A's ingest and title executions.
    expect(await activity.forgetUser(A)).toBe(3);

    for (const model of [INGEST_STATUS_MODEL, CHAT_ACTIVITY_MODEL]) {
      for (const r of await rows(model)) {
        const mine = r.id.startsWith(`u_${A}`);
        expect([r.id, r.erased, r.state === null]).toEqual([r.id, mine, mine]);
      }
    }
    expect((await rows(CHAT_ACTIVITY_MODEL)).find((r) => r.id === conv(B, chatB))?.state).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("is idempotent: a second call tombstones nothing", async () => {
    const { status, activity } = setup();
    await status.boot();
    await activity.boot();
    await status.forgetUser(A);
    await activity.forgetUser(A);
    expect(await status.forgetUser(A)).toBe(0);
    expect(await activity.forgetUser(A)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("a late event for a tombstoned row does not bring it back", async () => {
    const { store, log, status, activity } = setup();
    await status.boot();
    await activity.boot();
    await activity.forgetUser(A);
    const late = evt(chatConversationId(A, chatA), 1, "DECISION_RECORDED", {
      decisionId: "conv#o1:user",
      source: "conversation-record",
      choice: {},
    });
    log.append(late);
    activity.onEvent(late);
    await activity.drain();
    const [row] = foldedStates(await store.getFolded(CHAT_ACTIVITY_MODEL, [late.executionId]));
    expect(row).toBeUndefined(); // an erased row is hidden from readers
    const [raw] = await store.getFolded(CHAT_ACTIVITY_MODEL, [late.executionId]);
    expect(raw).toMatchObject({ state: null, erased: true });
  });

  it("drains folds already scheduled before it purges", async () => {
    const { log, status } = setup();
    await status.boot();
    const run = ingestRun(ingestOf(A, randomUUID()), "ready");
    log.append(...run);
    for (const e of run) status.onEvent(e); // scheduled, not awaited
    expect(await status.forgetUser(A)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4); // the new row is in the count
  });

  it("without a lister it purges by scope only (the previous behaviour)", async () => {
    const store = new InMemoryReadModelStore();
    const log = new MemoryLog();
    const status = new IngestStatus({
      writer: store,
      readerFor: () => store,
      source: log,
      sensitive: {},
      bus: new InMemoryProgressBus(),
    });
    log.append(...ingestRun(ingestOf(A, docA), "ready"), ...ingestRun(titleOf(A, chatA), "started"));
    await status.boot();
    expect(await status.forgetUser(A)).toBe(1);
  });

  it("refuses an id that is not a uuid, synchronously", () => {
    const { status, activity } = setup();
    expect(() => status.forgetUser("not-a-uuid")).toThrow(/forgetUser: userId must be a uuid/);
    expect(() => activity.forgetUser("x")).toThrow(/forgetUser: userId must be a uuid/);
  });
});

describe("forgetUserRows", () => {
  it("stops when a lister keeps returning rows the purge cannot tombstone", async () => {
    let listed = 0;
    const n = await forgetUserRows(
      { drain: async () => {}, purgeFolded: async () => 0 },
      "m",
      A,
      async () => {
        listed += 1;
        return ["u_x"];
      },
    );
    expect([n, listed]).toEqual([0, 1]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { ReadModelService } from "@loom/plugin-read-model";

/** The user's rows of a model that are not yet tombstoned (their execution ids, never their state). */
export type UserExecutionLister = (modelId: string, userId: string) => Promise<readonly string[]>;

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/;

/**
 * User erasure for one folded model, after the shred: tombstones every row of the user, never a delete, so the
 * watermark survives and a late event cannot bring one back.
 *
 * 1. Waits for the folds already scheduled (a live fold in flight could otherwise write a row after its tombstone).
 * 2. Purges by `scope` (the rows with the user as scope: one call).
 * 3. Purges by execution id every remaining row under the user's id prefix (`lister`): the neutral rows of the
 *    executions this model does not own, whose key and scope are NULL. Repeats while a pass tombstones something
 *    and the lister still finds rows, so a user with many rows needs no unbounded query.
 *
 * Without a `lister` it is the scope purge alone. Returns the number of rows newly tombstoned; a second call returns 0.
 * Throws a `TypeError` synchronously for a user id that is not a (lower-case) uuid.
 */
export function forgetUserRows(
  service: Pick<ReadModelService, "purgeFolded" | "drain">,
  modelId: string,
  userId: string,
  lister?: UserExecutionLister,
): Promise<number> {
  const scope = userId.toLowerCase();
  if (!UUID.test(scope)) throw new TypeError("forgetUser: userId must be a uuid");
  return (async () => {
    await service.drain();
    let total = await service.purgeFolded(modelId, { scope });
    if (!lister) return total;
    for (;;) {
      const ids = await lister(modelId, scope);
      let progress = 0;
      for (const executionId of ids) progress += await service.purgeFolded(modelId, { executionId });
      total += progress;
      if (ids.length === 0 || progress === 0) return total;
    }
  })();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the lister to `status/pgStores.ts`.**

```ts
import type { UserExecutionLister } from "./forgetUser";

const LIST_BATCH = 500;

/**
 * The Loom role's narrow read for user erasure: the execution ids (never the state) of one user's rows in a model
 * that are not tombstoned yet, by id prefix. The prefix is matched with `starts_with` and exact equality, never
 * `LIKE`, because `_` is a wildcard and a user id is bound text. The Loom pool's search_path is `loom`, so the
 * table is schema-qualified. `docmind_loom` may read every tenant's rows (policy `ingest_all`, migration 0012).
 */
export const pgUserExecutions =
  (loomPool: Pick<Pool, "query">): UserExecutionLister =>
  async (modelId, userId) => {
    const { rows } = await loomPool.query<{ execution_id: string }>(
      `SELECT execution_id FROM public.read_model_state
        WHERE model_id = $1 AND NOT erased
          AND (execution_id = $2 OR starts_with(execution_id, $3) OR starts_with(execution_id, $4))
        ORDER BY execution_id
        LIMIT ${LIST_BATCH}`,
      [modelId, `u_${userId}`, `u_${userId}:`, `u_${userId}.c-`],
    );
    return rows.map((r) => r.execution_id);
  };
```

### Task 168: `0024`: `erasure_requests`, `users.erasing_at`, the four definer functions; `ErasureStore`

Implements D2. The durable erasure request, and what the eraser and the account routes need from the database.

Two parts, two PRs:

- **168a: the migration.** The table (with `receipt_hash`), the column, the four erasure definer functions, and the two auth functions that refuse an erasing user.
- **168b: the store.** `ErasureStore` (with `status(receiptHash)`), `PgErasureStore`, an in-memory twin for the eraser's unit tests (Task 169), and `receipt.ts` (the token and its hash).

Design points the tests pin:

- **The request outlives the user.** No foreign key to `users` or `tenants`. It holds ids, a receipt hash, step names, a counter and times; no email, no content, and never the receipt token itself.
- **RLS stays on.** The repo's tenancy suite fails on any public table without it. The route inserts the request inside the user's own tenant transaction, so the app role gets `INSERT` only, under an insert policy whose check requires the row's tenant to be the transaction's tenant and the user to be one of that tenant's users, with `state 'pending'` and no steps. The app role can read and change the table only through the definer functions (`docmind_erasure_pending`, `docmind_erasure_mark`, `docmind_erasure_status`) (D2 as first drafted said "RLS is not applied"; see "Deviations recorded" in the header).
- **Four definer functions, ids and a state only.** `docmind_erasure_pending(p_state text DEFAULT 'pending')` (the requests in that state, across tenants, at most 1000; `'pending'` feeds the boot resume, `'done'` feeds the post-boot `forgetUser` re-run; any other state is rejected; STRICT, so NULL answers no rows); `docmind_erasure_mark(user, step, done)` (STRICT; records a step, or counts a retry); `docmind_erasure_status(p_receipt_hash bytea) RETURNS text` (STRICT; takes a hash and returns only `'pending'`, `'done'` or NULL; it is the sixth `docmind_` definer function and serves the unauthenticated receipt route of Task 170b); `docmind_delete_empty_tenant(tenant)` (STRICT; deletes a tenant that has no users left, the only way, since `docmind_app` cannot delete tenants). Each pins `search_path`, has `REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC`, and `GRANT EXECUTE` to `docmind_app`.
- **The receipt hash.** `receipt_hash bytea NOT NULL`, `UNIQUE` (constraint `erasure_requests_receipt_hash_key`) and exactly 32 bytes (`erasure_requests_receipt_hash_len`): the sha256 of the token text. The insert policy is unchanged (the hash is derived by the server and the app role cannot read the table back).
- **The email scrub needs nothing here.** The app role already holds `UPDATE` on `users` under the `tenant_isolation` policy of `0002` (the default privileges of `0001` grant it, and no migration revokes it), so the route's own tenant transaction can set `email = 'erased:' || id`. A test pins that, so a later migration that narrows `users` fails here instead of at runtime.
- **The auth functions refuse an erasing user.** `auth_find_login` returns no row and `auth_find_session` finds no session once `users.erasing_at` is set. Login then answers like an unknown email (the route's existing decoy path), and a session that slipped past the route's own delete cannot authenticate. Same signatures, so `CREATE OR REPLACE` keeps their grants.

**Files:**
- Create: `infra/migrations/0024_erasure.sql`
- Create: `apps/server/app/erasure/types.ts`, `apps/server/app/erasure/pgStore.ts`
- Modify: `apps/server/app/erasure/testing.ts` (`MemoryErasureStore`)
- Create: `apps/server/app/erasure/receipt.ts` (`newReceipt`, `hashReceipt`)
- Modify: `apps/server/app/documents/sweepSql.pg.test.ts` (the `DEFINERS` list)
- Test: `apps/server/app/db/erasureRequests.pg.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/erasure/pgStore.pg.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/erasure/memoryStore.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/erasure/receipt.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `0002`/`0003`: `tenants`, `users (tenant_id, id)`, `app_tenant_id()`, the `tenant_isolation` policies; `0005`: `auth_find_login`, `auth_find_session`; `0001`: default privileges (rewritten by the table's own `REVOKE`/`GRANT`).
  - `withTenant` (`db/tenant.ts`); the test kit `seedUsers`, `T1`, `T2`, `U1`, `U1B`, `U2` (`documents/testHarness.ts`).
- Produces:
  - Schema: `users.erasing_at timestamptz NULL`; `erasure_requests` (with `receipt_hash`); `docmind_erasure_pending(text)`, `docmind_erasure_mark(uuid, text, boolean)`, `docmind_erasure_status(bytea)`, `docmind_delete_empty_tenant(uuid)`.
  - `ERASURE_STEPS`, `type ErasureStep`, `isErasureStep`, `interface ErasureRequest`, `interface ErasureStore`, `class PgErasureStore`, `class MemoryErasureStore`, `newReceipt`, `hashReceipt` (the catalogue's names; additions: `ErasureStore.retried`, the required `ErasureStore.erased`, `insert`'s `receiptHash` and `status(receiptHash)`; see "Deviations recorded" in the header).

- [ ] **Step 1 (168a): Write the failing tests (`db/erasureRequests.pg.test.ts`).**

```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { Pool } from "pg";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ALICE, T1, T2, U1, U1B, U2, seedUsers } from "../documents/testHarness";
import { migrate, setAppRolePassword } from "./migrate";
import { withTenant } from "./tenant";
import { APP_PASSWORD, PG_URL, type TestDb, createTestDb } from "./testSupport";

const FUNCTIONS = [
  "docmind_delete_empty_tenant",
  "docmind_erasure_mark",
  "docmind_erasure_pending",
  "docmind_erasure_status",
];

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("erasure_requests and its functions (0024) (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let owner: Pool;
  let app: Pool;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    owner = db.owner;
    await migrate(owner);
    await setAppRolePassword(owner, APP_PASSWORD);
    await seedUsers(owner);
    app = new Pool({ connectionString: db.appUrl });
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await app?.end();
    await db?.drop();
  });

  // The receipt hash each request was made with, by user (the server derives it from a random token).
  const receipts = new Map<string, Buffer>();
  const newHash = () => createHash("sha256").update(randomBytes(32).toString("base64url")).digest();
  const request = (tenant: string, user: string) => {
    const hash = newHash();
    receipts.set(user, hash);
    return withTenant(app, tenant, (tx) =>
      tx.query("INSERT INTO erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id, receipt_hash) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [user, tenant, hash]),
    );
  };
  const stateOf = async (user: string) =>
    (await owner.query("SELECT state, steps, attempts, completed_at FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [user]))
      .rows[0];

  it("adds users.erasing_at, null for everyone", async () => {
    const { rows } = await owner.query("SELECT erasing_at FROM users");
    expect(rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(rows.every((r) => r.erasing_at === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  describe("the table", () => {
    it("lets a session insert its own tenant's user as pending, with no steps, and nothing else", async () => {
      await request(T1, U1);
      expect(await stateOf(U1)).toMatchObject({ state: "pending", steps: [], attempts: 0, completed_at: null });
    });

    it("refuses a request for another tenant, for a user who is not in the tenant, and for a pre-filled one", async () => {
      await expect(request(T1, U2)).rejects.toThrow(/row-level security/); // bob belongs to tenant 2
      await expect(request(T1, randomUUID())).rejects.toThrow(/row-level security/);
      const forged = (cols: string, vals: unknown[]) =>
        withTenant(app, T1, (tx) => tx.query(`INSERT INTO erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id, receipt_hash, ${cols}) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, ${vals.map((_, i) => `$${i + 4}`).join(", ")})`, [U1B, T1, newHash(), ...vals]));
      await expect(forged("state, completed_at", ["done", new Date()])).rejects.toThrow(/row-level security/);
      await expect(forged("steps", [["stop"]])).rejects.toThrow(/row-level security/);
    });

    it("a second insert for the same user is a no-op", async () => {
      await withTenant(app, T1, (tx) =>
        tx.query("INSERT INTO erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id, receipt_hash) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [U1, T1, newHash()]),
      );
      expect((await owner.query("SELECT 1 FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [U1])).rowCount).toBe(1);
      const stored = (await owner.query("SELECT receipt_hash FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [U1])).rows[0].receipt_hash;
      expect(stored).toEqual(receipts.get(U1)); // the first receipt stays the only one
    });

    it("keeps one request per receipt hash, and only a 32-byte hash", async () => {
      await expect(
        owner.query("INSERT INTO erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id, receipt_hash) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [randomUUID(), T1, receipts.get(U1)]),
      ).rejects.toThrow(/erasure_requests_receipt_hash_key/);
      await expect(
        owner.query("INSERT INTO erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id, receipt_hash) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [randomUUID(), T1, randomBytes(8)]),
      ).rejects.toThrow(/erasure_requests_receipt_hash_len/);
      await expect(
        owner.query("INSERT INTO erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [randomUUID(), T1]),
      ).rejects.toThrow(/null value in column "receipt_hash"/);
    });

    it("the app role cannot read, change or delete a request except through the functions", async () => {
      for (const sql of [
        "SELECT * FROM erasure_requests",
        "UPDATE erasure_requests SET state = 'done'",
        "DELETE FROM erasure_requests",
      ])
        await expect(withTenant(app, T1, (tx) => tx.query(sql))).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
    });

    it("refuses an unknown step in steps, and a done request without a completion time", async () => {
      await expect(owner.query("UPDATE erasure_requests SET steps = ARRAY['teleport'] WHERE user_id = $1", [U1])).rejects.toThrow(/erasure_requests_steps_check/);
      await expect(owner.query("UPDATE erasure_requests SET state = 'done' WHERE user_id = $1", [U1])).rejects.toThrow(/erasure_requests_done_pair/);
    });
  });

  describe("docmind_erasure_pending / docmind_erasure_mark", () => {
    it("lists the pending requests across tenants: ids, steps and a counter", async () => {
      await request(T2, U2);
      const { rows } = await app.query("SELECT * FROM docmind_erasure_pending()"); // the default state is 'pending'
      expect(rows.map((r) => r.user_id).sort()).toEqual([U1, U2].sort());
      expect(rows.find((r) => r.user_id === U2)).toEqual({ user_id: U2, tenant_id: T2, state: "pending", steps: [], attempts: 0 });
    });

    it("records a step once, in the order marked, and counts a retry without recording a step", async () => {
      const mark = async (step: string, done = false) =>
        (await app.query("SELECT docmind_erasure_mark($1, $2, $3) AS found", [U1, step, done])).rows[0].found;
      expect(await mark("stop")).toBe(true);
      expect(await mark("shred")).toBe(true);
      expect(await mark("stop")).toBe(true); // again: no duplicate
      expect(await mark("retry")).toBe(true);
      expect(await stateOf(U1)).toMatchObject({ steps: ["stop", "shred"], attempts: 1, state: "pending" });
    });

    it("done flips the state once and stamps a completion time that a repeat keeps", async () => {
      await app.query("SELECT docmind_erasure_mark($1, 'done', true)", [U1]);
      const first = (await stateOf(U1)).completed_at;
      expect(first).not.toBeNull();
      await app.query("SELECT docmind_erasure_mark($1, 'done', true)", [U1]);
      expect(await stateOf(U1)).toMatchObject({ state: "done", completed_at: first });
      const { rows } = await app.query("SELECT user_id FROM docmind_erasure_pending()");
      expect(rows.map((r) => r.user_id)).toEqual([U2]); // a done request is no longer listed as pending
      const done = await app.query("SELECT user_id, state FROM docmind_erasure_pending('done')");
      expect(done.rows).toEqual([{ user_id: U1, state: "done" }]); // ... and is listed when asked for 'done'
    });

    it("validates the state: only pending or done, and NULL answers no rows (STRICT)", async () => {
      await expect(app.query("SELECT * FROM docmind_erasure_pending('teleport')")).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "22023" });
      expect((await app.query("SELECT * FROM docmind_erasure_pending(NULL)")).rowCount).toBe(0);
    });

    it("answers false for a request that does not exist, rejects an unknown step, and is STRICT", async () => {
      expect((await app.query("SELECT docmind_erasure_mark($1, 'stop', false) AS f", [randomUUID()])).rows[0].f).toBe(false);
      await expect(app.query("SELECT docmind_erasure_mark($1, 'teleport', false)", [U2])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "22023" });
      expect((await app.query("SELECT docmind_erasure_mark(NULL, 'stop', false) AS f")).rows[0].f).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe("docmind_erasure_status", () => {
    const status = async (hash: Buffer | null) =>
      (await app.query("SELECT public.docmind_erasure_status($1) AS s", [hash])).rows[0].s;

    it("answers the state of the request a receipt hash belongs to, across tenants, and NULL for any other hash", async () => {
      expect(await status(receipts.get(U2) as Buffer)).toBe("pending"); // bob, tenant 2: the caller needs no tenant context
      expect(await status(receipts.get(U1) as Buffer)).toBe("done"); // finished above
      expect(await status(newHash())).toBeNull();
      expect(await status(Buffer.alloc(0))).toBeNull();
    });

    it("is STRICT: a NULL hash answers NULL, and it takes a hash and returns a state, nothing else", async () => {
      expect(await status(null)).toBeNull();
      const { rows } = await owner.query(
        "SELECT pg_get_function_arguments(oid) AS args, pg_get_function_result(oid) AS result FROM pg_proc WHERE proname = 'docmind_erasure_status'",
      );
      expect(rows).toEqual([{ args: "p_receipt_hash bytea", result: "text" }]);
    });
  });

  describe("docmind_delete_empty_tenant", () => {
    it("keeps a tenant that still has a user, deletes one that has none, and ignores NULL and unknown ids", async () => {
      const del = async (t: string | null) => (await app.query("SELECT docmind_delete_empty_tenant($1) AS gone", [t])).rows[0].gone;
      expect(await del(T1)).toBe(false);
      expect((await owner.query("SELECT 1 FROM tenants WHERE id = $1", [T1])).rowCount).toBe(1);
      await owner.query("DELETE FROM users WHERE tenant_id = $1", [T2]);
      expect(await del(T2)).toBe(true);
      expect((await owner.query("SELECT 1 FROM tenants WHERE id = $1", [T2])).rowCount).toBe(0);
      expect(await del(T2)).toBe(false); // again: gone already
      expect(await del(null)).toBeNull();
      expect(await del(randomUUID())).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe("a user who is being erased cannot log in or authenticate", () => {
    it("auth_find_login and auth_find_session stop answering once erasing_at is set, by the user's own transaction", async () => {
      await withTenant(app, T1, (tx) =>
        tx.query("INSERT INTO auth_sessions (token_hash, tenant_id, user_id, expires_at) VALUES ('live', $1, $2, now() + interval '1 day')", [T1, U1B]),
      );
      expect((await app.query("SELECT * FROM auth_find_login('alice2@example.com')")).rowCount).toBe(1);
      expect((await app.query("SELECT * FROM auth_find_session('live')")).rowCount).toBe(1);
      await withTenant(app, T1, (tx) => tx.query("UPDATE users SET erasing_at = now() WHERE id = $1", [U1B]));
      expect((await app.query("SELECT * FROM auth_find_login('alice2@example.com')")).rowCount).toBe(0);
      expect((await app.query("SELECT * FROM auth_find_session('live')")).rowCount).toBe(0);
      expect((await app.query("SELECT * FROM auth_find_login('alice@example.com')")).rowCount).toBe(1); // another user is unaffected
    });
  });

  it("the app role can rewrite users.email inside its own tenant transaction, and only there (the request-time scrub needs no function)", async () => {
    const scrub = (tenant: string, user: string) =>
      withTenant(app, tenant, (tx) => tx.query("UPDATE users SET email = 'erased:' || id::text WHERE id = $1", [user])) as Promise<{ rowCount: number }>;
    expect((await scrub(T1, U2)).rowCount).toBe(0); // bob belongs to tenant 2: RLS hides the row
    expect((await owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [U2])).rows[0].email).not.toMatch(/^erased:/);
    expect((await scrub(T1, U1B)).rowCount).toBe(1);
    expect((await owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [U1B])).rows[0].email).toBe(`erased:${U1B}`);
    expect((await owner.query("SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = 'alice2@example.com'")).rowCount).toBe(0); // the old address is free
  });

  it("the four definer functions pin search_path, are definers, are strict, and are executable by the app role alone", async () => {
    const { rows } = await owner.query(
      `SELECT p.proname, p.prosecdef, p.proisstrict, p.proconfig,
              pg_get_userbyid(p.proowner) AS owner,
              (SELECT array_agg(g ORDER BY g) FROM (
                 SELECT CASE WHEN a.grantee = 0 THEN 'PUBLIC' ELSE pg_get_userbyid(a.grantee) END || ':' || a.privilege_type AS g
                   FROM aclexplode(p.proacl) a) x) AS acl
         FROM pg_proc p JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace
        WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND p.proname = ANY ($1) ORDER BY p.proname`,
      [FUNCTIONS],
    );
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.proname)).toEqual(FUNCTIONS);
    for (const r of rows) {
      expect(r.prosecdef).toBe(true);
      expect(r.proconfig).toEqual(["search_path=pg_catalog, pg_temp"]);
      expect(r.acl).toEqual([`${r.owner}:EXECUTE`, "docmind_app:EXECUTE"].sort());
    }
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.proisstrict).map((r) => r.proname)).toEqual(FUNCTIONS); // each takes arguments, so each is STRICT
    void ALICE;
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3 (168a): Write `infra/migrations/0024_erasure.sql`.**

```sql
-- Account erasure (M5 D2). Three things:
--   * users.erasing_at: set in the transaction that accepts a DELETE /api/me; login and session lookup then refuse the user.
--   * erasure_requests: the durable request the eraser resumes from. It outlives the user (no foreign key), so it holds
--     ids, step names, a retry counter and times only: no email, no content.
--   * four narrow SECURITY DEFINER functions, the "fourth cross-tenant path": they list requests by state across tenants
--     (the boot resume has no tenant), record a step, answer the state of one request by the hash of its receipt (the
--     receipt route has no session, so no tenant), and delete a tenant that has no users left (docmind_app cannot
--     delete tenants, 0003). Each pins search_path, is STRICT where it takes arguments, returns ids or a state only, and
--     is executable by docmind_app alone.
--   * The email scrub (users.email = 'erased:' || id, done by the route in its own tenant transaction) needs nothing
--     here: docmind_app already holds UPDATE on users under the tenant_isolation policy.
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN erasing_at timestamptz;

CREATE TABLE erasure_requests (
  user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
  -- sha256 of the receipt token's text. The token itself is never stored; the hash is what the status route looks up.
  receipt_hash bytea NOT NULL
    CONSTRAINT erasure_requests_receipt_hash_key UNIQUE
    CONSTRAINT erasure_requests_receipt_hash_len CHECK (octet_length(receipt_hash) = 32),
  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
    CONSTRAINT erasure_requests_state_check CHECK (state IN ('pending', 'done')),
  steps text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
    CONSTRAINT erasure_requests_steps_check
    CHECK (steps <@ ARRAY['stop', 'shred', 'readModels', 'rows', 'blobs', 'key', 'done']::text[]),
  attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CONSTRAINT erasure_requests_attempts_check CHECK (attempts >= 0),
  requested_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  completed_at timestamptz,
  CONSTRAINT erasure_requests_done_pair CHECK ((state = 'done') = (completed_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX erasure_requests_pending_idx ON erasure_requests (requested_at) WHERE state = 'pending';

-- Row-level security stays on (the tenancy suite requires it on every table). The route inserts the request inside the
-- user's own tenant transaction, so the app role may INSERT and nothing else: the check pins the row to the
-- transaction's tenant, to a user of that tenant, and to a fresh pending request. Reads and updates go through the
-- functions below only.
ALTER TABLE erasure_requests ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON erasure_requests FROM PUBLIC, docmind_app;
GRANT INSERT ON erasure_requests TO docmind_app;
CREATE POLICY request_insert ON erasure_requests FOR INSERT TO docmind_app
  WITH CHECK (
    tenant_id = app_tenant_id()
    AND state = 'pending' AND steps = '{}' AND attempts = 0 AND completed_at IS NULL
    AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM users u WHERE u.id = user_id AND u.tenant_id = tenant_id));

-- The requests in one state ('pending', the default, or 'done'), across tenants: ids, step names and a counter. At most
-- 1000: pending oldest first (the boot resume), done newest first (the post-boot forgetUser re-run). Any other state is
-- rejected. STRICT, so a NULL state answers no rows. One function serves both lists, so the definer count is six (four erasure functions and the sweep's two).
CREATE FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_pending(p_state text DEFAULT 'pending')
RETURNS TABLE (user_id uuid, tenant_id uuid, state text, steps text[], attempts integer)
LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE STRICT SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp AS $$
BEGIN
  IF p_state NOT IN ('pending', 'done') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'unknown erasure state' USING ERRCODE = '22023';
  END IF;
  RETURN QUERY
    SELECT r.user_id, r.tenant_id, r.state, r.steps, r.attempts
      FROM public.erasure_requests r
     WHERE r.state = p_state
     ORDER BY (CASE WHEN p_state = 'pending' THEN r.requested_at END), r.completed_at DESC NULLS LAST, r.user_id
     LIMIT 1000;
END
$$;

-- Records one finished step of a request (once, in the order marked), or counts a retry (p_step = 'retry', which is not
-- a step). With p_done the request is done and its completion time is set once. False when there is no such request.
CREATE FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_mark(p_user_id uuid, p_step text, p_done boolean)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE plpgsql STRICT SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp AS $$
BEGIN
  IF p_step = 'retry' THEN
    UPDATE public.erasure_requests r
       SET attempts = r.attempts + 1, updated_at = pg_catalog.now()
     WHERE r.user_id = p_user_id AND r.state = 'pending';
    RETURN FOUND;
  END IF;
  IF NOT (p_step = ANY (ARRAY['stop', 'shred', 'readModels', 'rows', 'blobs', 'key', 'done']::text[])) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'unknown erasure step' USING ERRCODE = '22023';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.erasure_requests r
     SET steps = CASE WHEN p_step = ANY (r.steps) THEN r.steps ELSE pg_catalog.array_append(r.steps, p_step) END,
         state = CASE WHEN p_done THEN 'done' ELSE r.state END,
         completed_at = CASE WHEN p_done AND r.completed_at IS NULL THEN pg_catalog.now() ELSE r.completed_at END,
         updated_at = pg_catalog.now()
   WHERE r.user_id = p_user_id;
  RETURN FOUND;
END
$$;

-- The state of the request a receipt belongs to: 'pending', 'done', or NULL for a hash that matches none. It takes a hash
-- and returns a state, nothing else: no id, no step, no time. STRICT, so a NULL hash answers NULL. The receipt route has
-- no session and so no tenant; knowing the token (256 random bits) is the credential.
CREATE FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_status(p_receipt_hash bytea)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql STABLE STRICT SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp AS $$
  SELECT r.state FROM public.erasure_requests r WHERE r.receipt_hash = p_receipt_hash
$$;

-- Deletes a tenant that has no user left (the personal tenant of an erased user). docmind_app cannot delete tenants
-- (0003): a tenant's own session must never be able to cascade over its users. True when a tenant was deleted.
CREATE FUNCTION public.docmind_delete_empty_tenant(p_tenant_id uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql STRICT SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp AS $$
  WITH gone AS (
    DELETE FROM public.tenants t
     WHERE t.id = p_tenant_id
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.users u WHERE u.tenant_id = t.id)
    RETURNING 1)
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM gone)
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_pending(text) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_mark(uuid, text, boolean) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_status(bytea) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.docmind_delete_empty_tenant(uuid) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_pending(text) TO docmind_app;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_mark(uuid, text, boolean) TO docmind_app;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.docmind_erasure_status(bytea) TO docmind_app;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.docmind_delete_empty_tenant(uuid) TO docmind_app;

-- A user who is being erased cannot log in or authenticate. Same signatures as 0005, so the grants carry over; an
-- unknown email already takes the login route's decoy path, so the answer is the one for invalid credentials.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.auth_find_login(p_email text)
  RETURNS TABLE (user_id uuid, tenant_id uuid, password_hash text)
  LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp
  AS $$ SELECT u.id, u.tenant_id, u.password_hash FROM public.users u
         WHERE u.email = p_email AND u.erasing_at IS NULL $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.auth_find_session(p_token_hash text)
  RETURNS TABLE (user_id uuid, tenant_id uuid)
  LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp
  AS $$ SELECT s.user_id, s.tenant_id FROM public.auth_sessions s
          JOIN public.users u ON u.id = s.user_id AND u.tenant_id = s.tenant_id
         WHERE s.token_hash = p_token_hash AND s.expires_at > pg_catalog.now() AND u.erasing_at IS NULL $$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.auth_find_login(text) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.auth_find_session(text) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.auth_find_login(text) TO docmind_app;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.auth_find_session(text) TO docmind_app;
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MemoryErasureStore } from "./testing";
import { ERASURE_STEPS, isErasureStep } from "./types";

const [U, T] = [randomUUID(), randomUUID()];
const hash = (n: number) => Buffer.alloc(32, n);

describe("ERASURE_STEPS", () => {
  it("is the fixed order of D3, and the guard accepts exactly those", () => {
    expect(ERASURE_STEPS).toEqual(["stop", "shred", "readModels", "rows", "blobs", "key", "done"]);
    expect(ERASURE_STEPS.every(isErasureStep)).toBe(true);
    for (const x of ["retry", "", "STOP", 1, null]) expect(isErasureStep(x)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("MemoryErasureStore", () => {
  it("inserts pending, lists it, records a step once, and counts retries", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryErasureStore();
    await store.insert({}, U, T, hash(1));
    await store.insert({}, U, T, hash(2)); // idempotent: the first receipt stays
    expect(await store.status(hash(1))).toBe("pending");
    expect(await store.status(hash(2))).toBeNull();
    expect(await store.pending()).toEqual([{ userId: U, tenantId: T, state: "pending", steps: [], attempts: 0 }]);
    await store.mark(U, "stop");
    await store.mark(U, "stop");
    await store.retried(U);
    expect(await store.get(U)).toMatchObject({ steps: ["stop"], attempts: 1 });
  });

  it("done leaves the pending list, and get answers null for it and for a stranger", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryErasureStore();
    await store.insert({}, U, T, hash(1));
    await store.mark(U, "done");
    expect(await store.pending()).toEqual([]);
    expect(await store.get(U)).toBeNull();
    expect(await store.get(randomUUID())).toBeNull();
    expect(await store.status(hash(1))).toBe("done"); // the receipt keeps answering
    expect(await store.status(hash(9))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("erased() lists the done requests, newest first, and never a pending one", async () => {
    const store = new MemoryErasureStore();
    const [a, b, c] = [randomUUID(), randomUUID(), randomUUID()];
    for (const [i, u] of [a, b, c].entries()) await store.insert({}, u, T, hash(i + 1));
    expect(await store.erased()).toEqual([]);
    await store.mark(a, "done");
    await store.mark(b, "done");
    expect(await store.erased()).toEqual([b, a]);
    expect((await store.pending()).map((r) => r.userId)).toEqual([c]);
  });

  it("refuses to mark a request that does not exist", async () => {
    await expect(new MemoryErasureStore().mark(U, "stop")).rejects.toThrow(/no such erasure request/);
  });
});
```

`apps/server/app/erasure/receipt.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { hashReceipt, newReceipt } from "./receipt";

describe("receipt", () => {
  it("is 32 random bytes as 43 base64url characters, fresh every time", () => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
      const { token } = newReceipt();
      expect(token).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
      expect(Buffer.from(token, "base64url")).toHaveLength(32);
      seen.add(token);
    }
    expect(seen.size).toBe(50);
  });

  it("stores the sha256 of the token text and never the token", () => {
    const { token, hash } = newReceipt();
    expect(hash).toHaveLength(32);
    expect(hash.equals(createHash("sha256").update(token).digest())).toBe(true);
    expect(hashReceipt(token).equals(hash)).toBe(true);
    expect(hash.toString("base64url")).not.toBe(token);
    expect(hash.includes(Buffer.from(token))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`apps/server/app/erasure/pgStore.pg.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { Pool } from "pg";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { migrate, setAppRolePassword } from "../db/migrate";
import { withTenant } from "../db/tenant";
import { APP_PASSWORD, PG_URL, type TestDb, createTestDb } from "../db/testSupport";
import { T1, T2, U1, U1B, U2, seedUsers } from "../documents/testHarness";
import { PgErasureStore } from "./pgStore";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("PgErasureStore (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let app: Pool;
  let store: PgErasureStore;
  const hash = (name: string) => createHash("sha256").update(name).digest();

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    await migrate(db.owner);
    await setAppRolePassword(db.owner, APP_PASSWORD);
    await seedUsers(db.owner);
    app = new Pool({ connectionString: db.appUrl });
    store = new PgErasureStore(app);
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await app?.end();
    await db?.drop();
  });

  it("inserts inside the caller's own tenant transaction, and a second insert changes nothing", async () => {
    await withTenant(app, T1, (tx) => store.insert(tx, U1, T1, hash("u1")));
    await withTenant(app, T1, (tx) => store.insert(tx, U1, T1, hash("u1-again")));
    expect(await store.status(hash("u1-again"))).toBeNull(); // the first receipt is the only one
    expect(await store.get(U1)).toEqual({ userId: U1, tenantId: T1, state: "pending", steps: [], attempts: 0 });
  });

  it("refuses a request that names another tenant's user, and rolls the caller's transaction back with it", async () => {
    await expect(withTenant(app, T1, (tx) => store.insert(tx, U2, T1, hash("forged")))).rejects.toThrow(/row-level security/);
    expect(await store.get(U2)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("lists pending requests across tenants, records steps in the order marked, and counts retries", async () => {
    await withTenant(app, T2, (tx) => store.insert(tx, U2, T2, hash("u2")));
    expect((await store.pending()).map((r) => r.userId).sort()).toEqual([U1, U2].sort());
    for (const step of ["stop", "shred", "stop"] as const) await store.mark(U1, step);
    await store.retried(U1);
    expect(await store.get(U1)).toMatchObject({ steps: ["stop", "shred"], attempts: 1 });
  });

  it("status reads the receipt: pending, then done, null for any other hash", async () => {
    expect(await store.status(hash("u2"))).toBe("pending");
    expect(await store.status(hash("nobody"))).toBeNull();
    await store.mark(U2, "done");
    expect(await store.status(hash("u2"))).toBe("done");
    await store.mark(U2, "done");
  });

  it("done takes the request out of the pending list, and get answers null for it", async () => {
    await store.mark(U2, "done");
    expect(await store.get(U2)).toBeNull();
    expect((await store.pending()).map((r) => r.userId)).toEqual([U1]);
    expect(await store.erased()).toEqual([U2]); // erased() is the same function asked for 'done'
    const { rows } = await db.owner.query("SELECT state, steps FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [U2]);
    expect(rows[0]).toEqual({ state: "done", steps: ["done"] });
  });

  it("refuses to mark a request that does not exist, and carries no email, content or receipt token", async () => {
    await expect(store.mark(randomUUID(), "stop")).rejects.toThrow(/no such erasure request/);
    const { rows } = await db.owner.query(
      "SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = 'erasure_requests' ORDER BY column_name",
    );
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.column_name)).toEqual([
      "attempts", "completed_at", "receipt_hash", "requested_at", "state", "steps", "tenant_id", "updated_at", "user_id",
    ]);
    void U1B;
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8 (168b): Write `erasure/receipt.ts` and `erasure/types.ts`.** The receipt first:

```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";

/** The sha256 of the token's text. This, never the token, is what the database holds and what the status route looks up. */
export const hashReceipt = (token: string): Buffer => createHash("sha256").update(token, "utf8").digest();

/** A fresh receipt: 32 random bytes as base64url (43 characters), and its hash. The token is shown to the caller once. */
export function newReceipt(): { token: string; hash: Buffer } {
  const token = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
  return { token, hash: hashReceipt(token) };
}
```

  Then the types:

```ts
/** The steps of an erasure, in the order the eraser runs them (D3). `done` is the last mark, and finishes the request. */
export const ERASURE_STEPS = ["stop", "shred", "readModels", "rows", "blobs", "key", "done"] as const;
export type ErasureStep = (typeof ERASURE_STEPS)[number];

export const isErasureStep = (x: unknown): x is ErasureStep =>
  typeof x === "string" && (ERASURE_STEPS as readonly string[]).includes(x);

/** A durable erasure request: ids, the steps already finished, and a retry count. No email, no content. */
export interface ErasureRequest {
  userId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  state: "pending" | "done";
  steps: ErasureStep[];
  attempts: number;
}

/** Anything a statement can run on: a tenant transaction (`withTenant`'s client) or a test double. */
export interface Queryable {
  query(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>;
}

export type ErasureState = ErasureRequest["state"];

export interface ErasureStore {
  /**
   * Inserts a pending request inside the caller's tenant transaction (the route's, with the session delete and the
   * email scrub). `receiptHash` is the sha256 of the receipt token (`receipt.ts`); the token itself never reaches the
   * store. Idempotent: a second insert for the user changes nothing, the first receipt stays.
   */
  insert(tx: Queryable, userId: string, tenantId: string, receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<void>;
  /** The state of the request a receipt hash belongs to (`docmind_erasure_status`), or `null` for none. Done stays answerable. */
  status(receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<ErasureState | null>;
  /** Every pending request across tenants (the boot resume). */
  pending(): Promise<ErasureRequest[]>;
  /** Records a finished step once; `done` also finishes the request. Throws when there is no such request. */
  mark(userId: string, step: ErasureStep): Promise<void>;
  /** Counts a failed attempt (the eraser's backoff loop). Throws when there is no pending request. */
  retried(userId: string): Promise<void>;
  /** The user's PENDING request, or `null` (none, or already done: a re-run is a no-op). */
  get(userId: string): Promise<ErasureRequest | null>;
  /** The user ids of the requests that are done, newest first, at most 1000 (the post-boot `forgetUser` re-run). Required. */
  erased(): Promise<string[]>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9 (168b): Write `erasure/pgStore.ts`.**

```ts
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import {
  type ErasureRequest,
  type ErasureState,
  type ErasureStep,
  type ErasureStore,
  type Queryable,
  isErasureStep,
} from "./types";

interface Row {
  user_id: string;
  tenant_id: string;
  state: "pending" | "done";
  steps: string[];
  attempts: number;
}

const toRequest = (r: Row): ErasureRequest => ({
  userId: r.user_id,
  tenantId: r.tenant_id,
  state: r.state,
  steps: r.steps.filter(isErasureStep),
  attempts: r.attempts,
});

/**
 * The erasure requests on Postgres (migration 0024), connected as docmind_app. The table is reachable only through the
 * insert policy (inside a tenant transaction) and the definer functions (`docmind_erasure_pending(state)` serves both `pending()` and `erased()`,
 * `docmind_erasure_status(hash)` serves `status()`); nothing here reads or writes it directly but the insert. Every
 * statement carries ids, step names and a receipt hash only.
 */
export class PgErasureStore implements ErasureStore {
  constructor(private readonly pool: Pool) {}

  async insert(tx: Queryable, userId: string, tenantId: string, receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    // No conflict target: DO NOTHING needs the INSERT privilege only (the app role cannot SELECT the table).
    await tx.query(
      "INSERT INTO public.erasure_requests (user_id, tenant_id, receipt_hash) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [userId, tenantId, receiptHash],
    );
  }

  async status(receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<ErasureState | null> {
    const { rows } = await this.pool.query<{ state: string | null }>(
      "SELECT public.docmind_erasure_status($1) AS state",
      [receiptHash],
    );
    const state = rows[0]?.state;
    return state === "pending" || state === "done" ? state : null;
  }

  async pending(): Promise<ErasureRequest[]> {
    const { rows } = await this.pool.query<Row>(
      "SELECT user_id, tenant_id, state, steps, attempts FROM public.docmind_erasure_pending($1)",
      ["pending"],
    );
    return rows.map(toRequest);
  }

  async erased(): Promise<string[]> {
    const { rows } = await this.pool.query<Pick<Row, "user_id">>(
      "SELECT user_id FROM public.docmind_erasure_pending($1)",
      ["done"],
    );
    return rows.map((r) => r.user_id);
  }

  async get(userId: string): Promise<ErasureRequest | null> {
    return (await this.pending()).find((r) => r.userId === userId) ?? null;
  }

  async mark(userId: string, step: ErasureStep): Promise<void> {
    const { rows } = await this.pool.query<{ found: boolean }>(
      "SELECT public.docmind_erasure_mark($1, $2, $3) AS found",
      [userId, step, step === "done"],
    );
    if (!rows[0]?.found) throw new Error("no such erasure request");
  }

  async retried(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const { rows } = await this.pool.query<{ found: boolean }>(
      "SELECT public.docmind_erasure_mark($1, 'retry', false) AS found",
      [userId],
    );
    if (!rows[0]?.found) throw new Error("no such erasure request");
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10 (168b): Add `MemoryErasureStore` to `erasure/testing.ts`** (below `noSnapshots`; add `import { type ErasureRequest, type ErasureState, type ErasureStep, type ErasureStore, type Queryable } from "./types";` at the top).

```ts
/** The request store without a database, with the same answers as `PgErasureStore` (Task 169's unit tests use it). */
export class MemoryErasureStore implements ErasureStore {
  private readonly requests = new Map<string, ErasureRequest>();
  private readonly receipts = new Map<string, string>(); // receipt hash (hex) -> user id

  async insert(_tx: Queryable, userId: string, tenantId: string, receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    if (this.requests.has(userId)) return;
    this.requests.set(userId, { userId, tenantId, state: "pending", steps: [], attempts: 0 });
    this.receipts.set(receiptHash.toString("hex"), userId);
  }

  async status(receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<ErasureState | null> {
    const userId = this.receipts.get(receiptHash.toString("hex"));
    return (userId && this.requests.get(userId)?.state) || null;
  }

  async pending(): Promise<ErasureRequest[]> {
    return [...this.requests.values()].filter((r) => r.state === "pending").map((r) => ({ ...r, steps: [...r.steps] }));
  }

  async get(userId: string): Promise<ErasureRequest | null> {
    return (await this.pending()).find((r) => r.userId === userId) ?? null;
  }

  /** Done requests, newest first (insertion order reversed), at most 1000, like the definer function. */
  async erased(): Promise<string[]> {
    return [...this.requests.values()]
      .filter((r) => r.state === "done")
      .map((r) => r.userId)
      .reverse()
      .slice(0, 1000);
  }

  async mark(userId: string, step: ErasureStep): Promise<void> {
    const request = this.requests.get(userId);
    if (!request) throw new Error("no such erasure request");
    if (!request.steps.includes(step)) request.steps.push(step);
    if (step === "done") request.state = "done";
  }

  async retried(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const request = this.requests.get(userId);
    if (!request || request.state !== "pending") throw new Error("no such erasure request");
    request.attempts += 1;
  }
}
```

## Phase B: erasing

Phase A (Tasks 164 to 168) made erasure *possible*: the shred is wired and the subject index is hydrated at boot, read models survive a shredded log, both SSE groups can be closed per user, both read models can forget a user, and the durable `erasure_requests` row and its store exist. Phase B makes it *happen*: the `Eraser` runs the steps (Task 169), `DELETE /api/me` asks for it (Task 170), one end-to-end suite proves nothing of the user stays readable (Task 171), and the docs say what was built and what stays by design (Task 172).

Phase B assumptions (Phase A as written in Tasks 164 to 168, binding):
- `apps/server/app/erasure/types.ts` exports `ERASURE_STEPS`, `ErasureStep`, `ErasureRequest`, `Queryable`, `ErasureStore`; `erasure/pgStore.ts` exports `PgErasureStore` (`new PgErasureStore(pool)`, app pool); `erasure/testing.ts` exports `MemoryErasureStore` and `noSnapshots`. `ErasureStore` is `insert(tx: Queryable, userId, tenantId)` (idempotent), `pending()`, `mark(userId, step)` (`done` also finishes the request), `retried(userId)` (counts an attempt), `erased()` (**required**; the user ids of the done requests, newest first, at most 1000) and `get(userId)`, which answers **only for a pending request** (a done one is `null`, so a re-run is a no-op).
- Migration 0024: `users.erasing_at`; `erasure_requests` with RLS, INSERT-only for `docmind_app` (reads and updates go through `docmind_erasure_pending(p_state)`, which `pending()` calls with `'pending'` and `erased()` with `'done'`, and `docmind_erasure_mark`); `docmind_delete_empty_tenant(uuid)` returns boolean; `auth_find_login` and `auth_find_session` already refuse a user with `erasing_at` set.
- `app.eraseSubject(userId, undefined, { parked: "shred" })` works after Task 164. The tenant argument is **undefined**: DocMind has no `tenantOf`, so the subject index has one partition and a tenant id would answer `unknown`.
- `IngestStatus.forgetUser(userId)` and `ChatActivity.forgetUser(userId)` return `Promise<number>` (rows newly tombstoned), throw synchronously on a bad id, and tombstone by scope and by `u_<user>` prefix (Task 167). The documents event group and the chat stream group both have `closeUser(userId)` (Task 166).
- A rebuild or version bump (ingest-status `"4"`, chat-activity `"3"`) can re-create an erased execution's read-model row as `{}`. So boot re-runs `forgetUser` for every erasure that is done (Task 169 `resumeAll`), using the required `ErasureStore.erased()` of Task 168 (the user ids of done requests, at most 1000).

---

### Task 169: the `Eraser`: steps, single-flight, advisory lock, backoff, boot resume (spec §9 item 34, D3, D4)

Implements D3 and D4. `Eraser.run(userId)` drives one durable request through `stop`, `shred`, `readModels`, `rows`, `blobs`, `key`, `done`. Each step is idempotent and recorded through `ErasureStore.mark` when it succeeds, so a crash at any point resumes at the first unrecorded step. A failing step logs its name, the error class and the attempt number, waits (1 s, 2 s, 4 s, ... at most 60 s, unbounded attempts) and tries again; a later step never runs first. After `ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER = 10` failed attempts of one step the eraser also logs one warning line, `erasure stuck <step> <ErrorClass> attempts=<n>` (no ids), and repeats it every 10 further attempts, not on each one. `run` is single-flight per user in this process (a map) and across processes (a Postgres session advisory lock `erasure:<user>`). `resumeAll()` runs every pending request after boot has started recovery. `stop()` ends the backoff waits so shutdown never waits on an erasure that is waiting on a running chat turn.

Split seam: **169a** is the `Eraser` itself over narrow ports (steps, loop, single-flight, backoff, stop) with unit tests on fakes; **169b** is the Postgres implementation of the ports (work listing, advisory lock, row deletion, blobs), the wiring in `buildServer`, boot resume and the shutdown step. Merge 169a first; 169b is stacked.

**Files (169a):**
- Create: `apps/server/app/erasure/eraser.ts` (`Eraser`, `ErasureDeps`, `ErasureWork`, `STEP_ORDER`, `ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER`, `backoffMs`, `abortableSleep`, `ErasureParkedError`, `KeyNotDestroyedError`)
- Test: `apps/server/app/erasure/eraser.test.ts`

**Files (169b):**
- Create: `apps/server/app/erasure/pgSteps.ts` (`pgErasureSql`, `pgErasureLock`), `apps/server/app/erasure/wire.ts` (`erasureDeps`)
- Modify: `apps/server/app/compose.ts` (option `erasure`, builds the `Eraser`, returns `eraser`), `apps/server/app/bootIngest.ts` (`resumeAll` after recovery starts; `stopErasure` in the close sequence), `apps/server/app/closeSequence.ts` (`stopErasure`), `apps/server/app/setup.ts` (passes the Loom pool)
- Test: `apps/server/app/erasure/pgSteps.pg.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/closeSequence.test.ts` (one case), `apps/server/app/erasure/wire.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 168: `ErasureStore` (`pending`, `mark`, `retried`, `get`), `PgErasureStore`, `MemoryErasureStore`, `ErasureRequest`, `ErasureStep` (from `erasure/types.ts`, `pgStore.ts`, `testing.ts`), the definer function `docmind_delete_empty_tenant`, `users.erasing_at`.
  - Task 164: `app.eraseSubject(userId, undefined, { parked: "shred" })` returning `{ outcome: "erased" | "parked" | "unknown" }`.
  - Task 166: `closeUser(userId)` on `documentEventRoutes(...)` and `chatStreamRoutes(...)`.
  - Task 167: `IngestStatus.forgetUser(userId)`, `ChatActivity.forgetUser(userId)`.
  - Existing: `eraseUserBlobs(root, tenant, user)`; `IngestPort.cancel`; `StartScheduler.settle`; `StopRegistry.stop`; `Conversations.close`; `TitleStarter.idle`; `WorkflowRuntime.cancel`; `executionIdFor`; `chatConversationId`; `principalOf`; `withTenant`; `drainWithin`, `SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS`; `closeSequence`.
- Produces:
  - `ErasureDeps` (below), `Eraser { run(userId): Promise<void>; resumeAll(): Promise<void>; idle(): Promise<void>; stop(): Promise<void> }`. `run` never rejects. `stop()` is an addition to the catalogue (shutdown needs it; `idle()` alone would wait on a backoff).
  - `STEP_ORDER`, `ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER`, `backoffMs(attempt)`, `abortableSleep(ms, signal)`; `ErasureDeps.log?(line: string)` (the stuck line; defaults to `console.warn`).
  - `pgErasureSql({ app, loom, blobRoot })` returning `{ lock, work, rows, blobs }`; `pgErasureLock(pool)`.
  - `erasureDeps(parts): ErasureDeps` (the production wiring).
  - `ComposeOptions.erasure?: { loomPool: Pool; wrap?: (d: ErasureDeps) => ErasureDeps; sleep?: ErasureDeps["sleep"] }` and `buildServer(...)` returns `eraser?: Eraser`. `wrap` and `sleep` are test seams (the crash test of Task 171 and fast backoff). Absent: no eraser and no `DELETE /api/me` (the CLI, most tests).

- [ ] **Step 1 (169a): Write the failing unit tests** (`erasure/eraser.test.ts`). The ports are fakes that append to one `calls` list, so the order of the whole erasure is one array.

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER,
  Eraser,
  type ErasureDeps,
  ErasureParkedError,
  STEP_ORDER,
  abortableSleep,
  backoffMs,
} from "./eraser";
import { MemoryErasureStore } from "./testing";
import type { ErasureRequest, ErasureStep } from "./types";

const USER = "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111";
const TENANT = "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222";
const DOC = "33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333";
const CHAT = "44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444";
const EMAIL = "erase-me@example.com";

const request = (steps: ErasureStep[] = [], state: "pending" | "done" = "pending"): ErasureRequest => ({
  userId: USER,
  tenantId: TENANT,
  state,
  steps,
  attempts: 0,
});

/**
 * The real in-memory store (Task 168), seeded with the given requests, plus what the unit tests also need:
 * every step ever marked (a done request reads `null` from `get`), and the retries counted.
 */
function memoryStore(initial: ErasureRequest[], erased: string[] = []) {
  const inner = new MemoryErasureStore();
  const marked = new Map<string, ErasureStep[]>();
  const retries = new Map<string, number>();
  const tx = { query: async () => undefined };
  const ready = (async () => {
    for (const [i, r] of initial.entries()) {
      await inner.insert(tx, r.userId, r.tenantId, Buffer.alloc(32, i + 1)); // a distinct receipt hash per request
      for (const step of r.steps) await inner.mark(r.userId, step);
    }
  })();
  return {
    marked,
    retries,
    get: async (userId: string) => (await ready, inner.get(userId)),
    pending: async () => (await ready, inner.pending()),
    async mark(userId: string, step: ErasureStep) {
      await ready;
      marked.set(userId, [...(marked.get(userId) ?? []), step]);
      return inner.mark(userId, step);
    },
    async retried(userId: string) {
      await ready;
      retries.set(userId, (retries.get(userId) ?? 0) + 1);
      return inner.retried(userId);
    },
    erased: async () => (await ready, [...erased, ...(await inner.erased())]),
  };
}

interface Knobs {
  outcomes?: ("erased" | "parked" | "unknown")[];
  destroyed?: boolean;
  failOnce?: Partial<Record<string, number>>;
  lock?: boolean;
}

function fakeDeps(initial: ErasureRequest[], k: Knobs = {}, erased: string[] = []) {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const lines: string[] = []; // what the fake `log` received: the stuck warnings
  const delays: number[] = [];
  const outcomes = [...(k.outcomes ?? ["erased"])];
  const failOnce = { ...(k.failOnce ?? {}) };
  const store = memoryStore(initial, erased);
  const note = (c: string) => calls.push(c);
  const maybeFail = (name: string) => {
    if ((failOnce[name] ?? 0) > 0) {
      failOnce[name] = (failOnce[name] ?? 0) - 1;
      throw new TypeError(`boom ${EMAIL}`); // a message with an email: the log must carry the class only
    }
  };
  const deps: ErasureDeps = {
    store,
    lock: async () => {
      if (k.lock === false) return null;
      note("lock");
      return async () => void note("unlock");
    },
    work: async () => {
      note("work");
      return { documents: [{ id: DOC, generation: 2 }], chatIds: [CHAT] };
    },
    closeStreams: (u) => void note(`closeStreams:${u === USER}`),
    settleStart: async (_i, d) => void note(`settleStart:${d === DOC}`),
    settleTitles: async () => void note("settleTitles"),
    cancelIngest: async (_i, d, g) => void note(`cancelIngest:${d === DOC}:${g}`),
    stopChat: async (_i, c) => void note(`stopChat:${c === CHAT}`),
    cancelTitle: async (_i, c) => void note(`cancelTitle:${c === CHAT}`),
    eraseSubject: async () => {
      note("eraseSubject");
      maybeFail("eraseSubject");
      return { outcome: outcomes.length > 1 ? (outcomes.shift() as "parked") : (outcomes[0] ?? "erased") };
    },
    keys: {
      destroy: async () => void note("keys.destroy"),
      wasDestroyed: async () => k.destroyed ?? true,
    },
    forgetStatus: async () => void note("forgetStatus"),
    forgetActivity: async () => void note("forgetActivity"),
    rows: async () => {
      note("rows");
      maybeFail("rows");
    },
    blobs: async () => void note("blobs"),
    log: (line) => void lines.push(line),
    // Records the wait and yields a macrotask, so a retry loop never starves the test's own timers.
    sleep: async (ms) => {
      delays.push(ms);
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1));
    },
  };
  return { deps, calls, delays, lines, store };
}

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

describe("Eraser", () => {
  it("runs the steps in order, records each, and ends with the request done", async () => {
    const { deps, calls, store } = fakeDeps([request()]);
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      "lock",
      "closeStreams:true",
      "work",
      "settleStart:true",
      "cancelIngest:true:2",
      "stopChat:true",
      "cancelTitle:true",
      "settleTitles",
      "eraseSubject",
      "forgetStatus",
      "forgetActivity",
      "rows",
      "blobs",
      "keys.destroy",
      "blobs", // again after the key is gone: an upload that raced the erase
      "unlock",
    ]);
    expect(await store.get(USER)).toBeNull(); // done
    expect(store.marked.get(USER)).toEqual([...STEP_ORDER]);
  });

  it("resumes at the first unrecorded step", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([request(["stop", "shred"])]);
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(calls[0]).toBe("lock");
    expect(calls[1]).toBe("forgetStatus");
    expect(calls).not.toContain("eraseSubject");
    expect(calls).not.toContain("closeStreams:true");
  });

  it("does nothing for a request that is done or unknown", async () => {
    const done = fakeDeps([request([...STEP_ORDER], "done")]);
    await new Eraser(done.deps).run(USER);
    expect(done.calls).toEqual(["lock", "unlock"]);
    const none = fakeDeps([]);
    await new Eraser(none.deps).run(USER);
    expect(none.calls).toEqual(["lock", "unlock"]);
  });

  it("retries a parked shred with growing waits and stops the work again before each retry", async () => {
    const { deps, calls, delays } = fakeDeps([request()], { outcomes: ["parked", "parked", "erased"] });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(delays).toEqual([1_000, 2_000]);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c === "eraseSubject")).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c === "stopChat:true")).toHaveLength(3); // the stop step, then once per parked answer
    expect(calls.indexOf("forgetStatus")).toBeGreaterThan(calls.lastIndexOf("eraseSubject"));
  });

  it("accepts 'unknown' (the user owns no execution)", async () => {
    const { deps, store } = fakeDeps([request()], { outcomes: ["unknown"] });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(await store.get(USER)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("does not move past a shred whose key is still alive, and logs the class and attempt only", async () => {
    const errors = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { deps, calls, delays, store } = fakeDeps([request()], { destroyed: false });
    const eraser = new Eraser(deps);
    const run = eraser.run(USER);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(delays.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2));
    await eraser.stop();
    await run;
    expect(calls).not.toContain("forgetStatus");
    expect((await store.get(USER))?.steps).toEqual(["stop"]);
    expect(errors).toHaveBeenCalledWith("erasure step failed", "shred", "KeyNotDestroyedError", 1);
    expect(errors).toHaveBeenCalledWith("erasure step failed", "shred", "KeyNotDestroyedError", 2);
  });

  it("logs the error class, never the message", async () => {
    const errors = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { deps, store } = fakeDeps([request()], { failOnce: { rows: 1 } });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(store.retries.get(USER)).toBe(1); // the attempt is counted on the durable request
    const logged = JSON.stringify(errors.mock.calls);
    expect(logged).toContain("TypeError");
    expect(logged).not.toContain(EMAIL);
    expect(logged).not.toContain(USER);
    expect(errors).toHaveBeenCalledWith("erasure step failed", "rows", "TypeError", 1);
  });

  it("retries a failed step before any later step runs", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([request()], { failOnce: { rows: 2 } });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    const afterShred = calls.slice(calls.indexOf("forgetActivity") + 1);
    expect(afterShred.slice(0, 4)).toEqual(["rows", "rows", "rows", "blobs"]);
  });

  it("warns once when a step has failed ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER times, then every ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER further attempts, not on each", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    expect(ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER).toBe(10);
    const { deps, lines, store } = fakeDeps([request()], { failOnce: { rows: 25 } });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(store.retries.get(USER)).toBe(25); // 25 failed attempts, then the 26th succeeded
    expect(lines).toEqual([
      "erasure stuck rows TypeError attempts=10",
      "erasure stuck rows TypeError attempts=20",
    ]);
  });

  it("stays quiet below the threshold, counts each step on its own, and never names an id, an email or a message", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const quiet = fakeDeps([request()], { failOnce: { eraseSubject: 9, rows: 9 } });
    await new Eraser(quiet.deps).run(USER);
    expect(quiet.lines).toEqual([]); // 9 + 9 failures, but never 10 of one step
    const { deps, lines } = fakeDeps([request()], { failOnce: { rows: 10 } });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lines[0]).toMatch(/^erasure stuck rows TypeError attempts=10$/);
    for (const secret of [USER, TENANT, DOC, CHAT, EMAIL]) expect(lines.join("\n")).not.toContain(secret);
  });

  it("warns for a shred that stays parked, with the class of the parked error", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { deps, lines } = fakeDeps([request()], { outcomes: [...Array(10).fill("parked"), "erased"] });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(lines).toEqual(["erasure stuck shred ErasureParkedError attempts=10"]);
  });

  it("is single-flight per user", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([request()]);
    const eraser = new Eraser(deps);
    const a = eraser.run(USER);
    const b = eraser.run(USER);
    expect(b).toBe(a);
    await a;
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c === "eraseSubject")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(calls.filter((c) => c === "lock")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("does nothing when another process holds the user's lock", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([request()], { lock: false });
    await new Eraser(deps).run(USER);
    expect(calls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("stop() ends a backoff wait, and run() resolves without finishing", async () => {
    const { deps, store } = fakeDeps([request()], { outcomes: ["parked"] });
    const real = { ...deps, sleep: abortableSleep };
    const eraser = new Eraser(real);
    const run = eraser.run(USER);
    await vi.waitFor(async () => expect((await store.get(USER))?.steps).toEqual(["stop"]));
    await eraser.stop();
    await run;
    await eraser.idle();
    expect((await store.get(USER))?.state).toBe("pending");
  });

  it("resumeAll starts every pending request and none of the done ones", async () => {
    const other = { ...request(), userId: "55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555555" };
    const { deps, store } = fakeDeps([request(), other, { ...request([...STEP_ORDER], "done"), userId: "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666666" }]);
    const eraser = new Eraser(deps);
    await eraser.resumeAll();
    await eraser.idle();
    expect(await store.get(USER)).toBeNull();
    expect(await store.get(other.userId)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("resumeAll survives a store that cannot list", async () => {
    const errors = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { deps } = fakeDeps([request()]);
    deps.store.pending = async () => {
      throw new RangeError("db down");
    };
    await expect(new Eraser(deps).resumeAll()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(errors).toHaveBeenCalledWith("erasure step failed", "resume", "RangeError", 1);
  });

  it("resumeAll re-forgets the read-model rows of every erasure that is done (a rebuild can re-create them as {})", async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], {}, ["77777777-7777-4777-8777-777777777777"]);
    const eraser = new Eraser(deps);
    await eraser.resumeAll();
    await eraser.idle();
    expect(calls).toEqual(["forgetStatus", "forgetActivity"]);
  });

  it("backs off 1 s, 2 s, 4 s ... capped at 60 s", () => {
    expect([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 50].map(backoffMs)).toEqual([
      1_000, 2_000, 4_000, 8_000, 16_000, 32_000, 60_000, 60_000, 60_000,
    ]);
  });

  it("names a parked shred", () => {
    expect(new ErasureParkedError().name).toBe("ErasureParkedError");
  });
});
```
