- [ ] **Step 3 (169a): Write the `Eraser`** (`erasure/eraser.ts`).

```ts
import type { Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import { SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS, drainWithin } from "../shutdown";
import type { ErasureRequest, ErasureStep, ErasureStore } from "./types";

/** The order is the contract (D3): a step never runs before the one above it is recorded. */
export const STEP_ORDER: readonly ErasureStep[] = [
  "stop",
  "shred",
  "readModels",
  "rows",
  "blobs",
  "key",
  "done",
];

/**
 * Failed attempts of one step between two "stuck" warnings: the first at attempt 10, then 20, 30, ... Not every
 * attempt, so a step that retries for hours writes a handful of lines, not thousands.
 */
export const ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER = 10;

/** 1 s, 2 s, 4 s ... at most 60 s. `attempt` counts from 1. */
export const backoffMs = (attempt: number): number =>
  Math.min(60_000, 1_000 * 2 ** Math.min(Math.max(attempt, 1) - 1, 6));

/** Resolves after `ms`, or at once when `signal` aborts. Never rejects. */
export function abortableSleep(ms: number, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (signal.aborted) return resolve();
    const finish = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      signal.removeEventListener("abort", finish);
      resolve();
    };
    const timer = setTimeout(finish, ms);
    signal.addEventListener("abort", finish, { once: true });
  });
}

/** A running chat turn (or ingest step) still owns an execution: the shred must wait for it. */
export class ErasureParkedError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("an execution of the user is still running");
    this.name = "ErasureParkedError";
  }
}

/** The shred reported the user erased but the key store still has a live key. */
export class KeyNotDestroyedError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("the user's key is still alive after the shred");
    this.name = "KeyNotDestroyedError";
  }
}

/** What the user owns that the `stop` step must wind down. Ids only. */
export interface ErasureWork {
  documents: { id: string; generation: number }[];
  /** Every chat id, soft-deleted ones included. */
  chatIds: string[];
}

/**
 * Every collaborator the steps need, as the narrowest function each. `wire.ts` builds the real ones;
 * the unit tests build fakes.
 */
export interface ErasureDeps {
  store: Pick<ErasureStore, "pending" | "erased" | "mark" | "retried" | "get">;
  /** The cross-process lock for one user; null when another process holds it. The returned function releases it. */
  lock(userId: string): Promise<(() => Promise<void>) | null>;
  /** The user's documents (with their ingest generation) and chats, read under the tenant. */
  work(req: ErasureRequest): Promise<ErasureWork>;
  /** Ends the user's open document and chat streams. */
  closeStreams(userId: string): void;
  /** Waits (bounded) for the document's pending ingest start, so a cancel never runs before it lands. */
  settleStart(identity: Identity, documentId: string): Promise<void>;
  settleTitles(): Promise<void>;
  cancelIngest(identity: Identity, documentId: string, generation: number): Promise<void>;
  /** Fires the chat's stop signal, then closes its conversation; tolerates a conversation that is gone. */
  stopChat(identity: Identity, chatId: string): Promise<void>;
  cancelTitle(identity: Identity, chatId: string): Promise<void>;
  /**
   * `app.eraseSubject(userId, undefined, { parked: "shred" })`. The tenant argument is undefined on purpose:
   * DocMind has no `tenantOf`, so Loom's subject index has one partition, and a tenant id would answer `unknown`.
   */
  eraseSubject(
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<{ outcome: "erased" | "parked" | "unknown" }>;
  keys: {
    destroy(subjectId: string): Promise<void>;
    wasDestroyed(subjectId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  };
  forgetStatus(userId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  forgetActivity(userId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  /** The user row (cascade), leftover Loom timers and leases, the empty personal tenant. */
  rows(req: ErasureRequest): Promise<void>;
  blobs(req: ErasureRequest): Promise<void>;
  /** Test seam: the backoff wait. Defaults to `abortableSleep`. */
  sleep?(ms: number, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
  /** Where the stuck warning goes (one line, no ids). Defaults to `console.warn`. */
  log?(line: string): void;
}

const classOf = (err: unknown) => (err instanceof Error ? err.constructor.name : typeof err);

/**
 * Runs durable erasure requests (D3, D4). It logs a step's name, the error class and the attempt (and, every
 * `ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER` failed attempts of one step, the stuck line), and nothing else: no user id, email, title,
 * file name, key, receipt or connection string.
 */
export class Eraser {
  private readonly runs = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
  private readonly halt = new AbortController();

  constructor(private readonly deps: ErasureDeps) {}

  /** Erases the user, or resumes the erasure. Single-flight per user; resolves when done or stopped; never rejects. */
  run(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const live = this.runs.get(userId);
    if (live) return live;
    const run: Promise<void> = this.drive(userId)
      .catch((err: unknown) => this.report("run", err, 1))
      .finally(() => {
        if (this.runs.get(userId) === run) this.runs.delete(userId);
      });
    this.runs.set(userId, run);
    return run;
  }

  /** Starts every pending request (boot). Does not wait for them: the server must not wait to listen. */
  async resumeAll(): Promise<void> {
    let pending: ErasureRequest[];
    try {
      pending = await this.deps.store.pending();
    } catch (err) {
      this.report("resume", err, 1);
      return;
    }
    for (const request of pending) void this.run(request.userId);
    // Finished erasures: a rebuild or version bump re-creates an erased execution's read-model row as `{}`.
    // Forgetting is idempotent and cheap (it lists only rows that are not tombstoned yet). Always runs, after the
    // read models have booted (this is called from `afterListen`).
    try {
      for (const userId of await this.deps.store.erased()) {
        await this.deps.forgetStatus(userId);
        await this.deps.forgetActivity(userId);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.report("resume", err, 1);
    }
  }

  /** Resolves when every run started so far has settled (tests). */
  async idle(): Promise<void> {
    while (this.runs.size > 0) await Promise.all([...this.runs.values()]);
  }

  /** Shutdown: ends every backoff wait, lets a step already running finish (bounded), starts no new step. */
  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    this.halt.abort();
    await drainWithin(this.idle(), SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);
  }

  private async drive(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const release = await this.acquire(userId);
    if (!release) return;
    try {
      const req = await this.deps.store.get(userId);
      if (!req || req.state === "done") return;
      const recorded = new Set<ErasureStep>(req.steps);
      for (const step of STEP_ORDER) {
        if (recorded.has(step)) continue;
        if (!(await this.attempt(req, step))) return; // stopped
        recorded.add(step);
      }
    } finally {
      await release().catch(() => undefined);
    }
  }

  private async acquire(userId: string): Promise<(() => Promise<void>) | null> {
    for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt++) {
      if (this.halt.signal.aborted) return null;
      try {
        return await this.deps.lock(userId);
      } catch (err) {
        this.report("lock", err, attempt);
        if (!(await this.pause(attempt))) return null;
      }
    }
  }

  /** One step until it succeeds and is recorded. False when the eraser was stopped first. */
  private async attempt(req: ErasureRequest, step: ErasureStep): Promise<boolean> {
    for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt++) {
      if (this.halt.signal.aborted) return false;
      try {
        await this.step(req, step);
        await this.deps.store.mark(req.userId, step);
        return true;
      } catch (err) {
        this.report(step, err, attempt);
        if (attempt % ERASURE_STUCK_AFTER === 0) this.warnStuck(step, err, attempt);
        await this.deps.store.retried(req.userId).catch(() => undefined); // best effort: the count is for operators
        if (!(await this.pause(attempt))) return false;
      }
    }
  }

  private async pause(attempt: number): Promise<boolean> {
    await (this.deps.sleep ?? abortableSleep)(backoffMs(attempt), this.halt.signal);
    return !this.halt.signal.aborted;
  }

  private report(step: string, err: unknown, attempt: number): void {
    console.error("erasure step failed", step, classOf(err), attempt);
  }

  /** One line for an operator: the step, the error class and the attempt count. Never an id or a message. */
  private warnStuck(step: ErasureStep, err: unknown, attempt: number): void {
    const line = `erasure stuck ${step} ${classOf(err)} attempts=${attempt}`;
    (this.deps.log ?? ((l: string) => console.warn(l)))(line);
  }

  private async step(req: ErasureRequest, step: ErasureStep): Promise<void> {
    const { deps } = this;
    switch (step) {
      case "stop":
        return this.stopWork(req);
      case "shred": {
        const { outcome } = await deps.eraseSubject(req.userId);
        if (outcome === "parked") {
          // A turn is still running. Stop whatever started since, then wait and ask again.
          await this.stopWork(req);
          throw new ErasureParkedError();
        }
        // 'unknown': the user owns no execution. 'erased': the shred destroyed the key itself (E2).
        if (outcome === "erased" && !(await deps.keys.wasDestroyed(req.userId)))
          throw new KeyNotDestroyedError();
        return;
      }
      case "readModels":
        await deps.forgetStatus(req.userId);
        await deps.forgetActivity(req.userId);
        return;
      case "rows":
        return deps.rows(req);
      case "blobs":
        return deps.blobs(req);
      case "key":
        // Idempotent; covers a user the shred never saw. Then the blobs once more: an upload that was
        // already streaming when the erase began may have landed after the first sweep (E9).
        await deps.keys.destroy(req.userId);
        if (!(await deps.keys.wasDestroyed(req.userId))) throw new KeyNotDestroyedError();
        return deps.blobs(req);
      case "done":
        return; // `mark` records the state
    }
  }

  /** Step 1: nothing of the user may still be running or startable. Idempotent, and safe to repeat. */
  private async stopWork(req: ErasureRequest): Promise<void> {
    const { deps } = this;
    const identity: Identity = { tenantId: req.tenantId, userId: req.userId };
    deps.closeStreams(req.userId);
    const work = await deps.work(req);
    for (const doc of work.documents) {
      await deps.settleStart(identity, doc.id);
      await deps.cancelIngest(identity, doc.id, doc.generation);
    }
    for (const chatId of work.chatIds) {
      await deps.stopChat(identity, chatId);
      await deps.cancelTitle(identity, chatId);
    }
    await deps.settleTitles();
  }
}
```

```ts
import { createSecretKey, randomBytes, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { PostgresKeyStore } from "@docmind/keystore-pg";
import { Pool } from "pg";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { migrate, setAppRolePassword, setRolePassword } from "../db/migrate";
import { APP_PASSWORD, LOOM_PASSWORD, PG_URL, type TestDb, createTestDb } from "../db/testSupport";
import { type DurableStorage, openDurableStorage } from "../storage";
import { pgErasureLock, pgErasureSql } from "./pgSteps";
import type { ErasureRequest } from "./types";

const HASH = "$argon2id$not-a-real-hash";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("the Postgres steps of the eraser (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let app: Pool;
  let durable: DurableStorage;
  let root: string;
  let sql: ReturnType<typeof pgErasureSql>;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    await migrate(db.owner);
    await setAppRolePassword(db.owner, APP_PASSWORD);
    await setRolePassword(db.owner, "docmind_loom", LOOM_PASSWORD);
    app = new Pool({ connectionString: db.appUrl });
    const keys = new PostgresKeyStore(app, [{ id: "kek-1", key: createSecretKey(randomBytes(32)) }]);
    durable = await openDurableStorage({ loomUrl: db.loomUrl, keys });
    root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "dm-erasure-pg-"));
    sql = pgErasureSql({ app, loom: durable.pool, blobRoot: root });
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await durable?.close();
    await app?.end();
    await db?.drop();
    if (root) rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  /** A tenant with the given users (sign-up makes one user per tenant; more go in as the owner does). */
  async function tenantWith(...emails: string[]) {
    const tenantId = randomUUID();
    const users: { userId: string; email: string }[] = [];
    for (const [i, email] of emails.entries()) {
      const userId = randomUUID();
      if (i === 0) await db.owner.query("SELECT public.auth_sign_up($1, $2, $3, $4)", [tenantId, userId, email, HASH]);
      else await db.owner.query("INSERT INTO users (id, tenant_id, email, password_hash) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)", [userId, tenantId, email, HASH]);
      users.push({ userId, email });
    }
    return { tenantId, users };
  }
  const request = (tenantId: string, userId: string): ErasureRequest => ({
    userId,
    tenantId,
    state: "pending",
    steps: [],
    attempts: 0,
  });
  const count = async (q: string, args: unknown[]) => (await db.owner.query(q, args)).rows[0].n as number;

  it("lists the user's chats, soft-deleted ones included, and never a colleague's", async () => {
    const { tenantId, users } = await tenantWith("a@work.test", "b@work.test");
    const [a, b] = users as [(typeof users)[0], (typeof users)[0]];
    const mine = [randomUUID(), randomUUID()];
    for (const id of mine)
      await db.owner.query("INSERT INTO chat_sessions (id, tenant_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [id, tenantId, a.userId]);
    await db.owner.query("UPDATE chat_sessions SET deleted_at = now() WHERE id = $1", [mine[1]]);
    await db.owner.query("INSERT INTO chat_sessions (id, tenant_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [randomUUID(), tenantId, b.userId]);
    const work = await sql.work(request(tenantId, a.userId));
    expect([...work.chatIds].sort()).toEqual([...mine].sort());
    expect(work.documents).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("rows: deletes the user (whose email is already scrubbed) and their data, the user's timers and leases, and the empty tenant; twice is fine", async () => {
    const { tenantId, users } = await tenantWith("solo@erase.test");
    const [u] = users as [(typeof users)[0]];
    // The request transaction (Task 170a) has already replaced the email; the step must not need it.
    await db.owner.query("UPDATE users SET email = 'erased:' || id::text, erasing_at = now() WHERE id = $1", [u.userId]);
    const chatId = randomUUID();
    await db.owner.query("INSERT INTO chat_sessions (id, tenant_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [chatId, tenantId, u.userId]);
    const exec = `u_${u.userId}:ingest-${randomUUID()}.2`;
    const other = `u_${randomUUID()}:ingest-${randomUUID()}.2`;
    for (const id of [exec, other]) {
      await durable.pool.query("INSERT INTO loom.timers (execution_id, wake_at) VALUES ($1, 1)", [id]);
      await durable.pool.query("INSERT INTO loom.leases (execution_id, owner, expires_at) VALUES ($1, 'x', 1)", [id]);
    }

    await sql.rows(request(tenantId, u.userId));
    await sql.rows(request(tenantId, u.userId)); // idempotent: nothing left to delete is not an error

    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM users WHERE id = $1", [u.userId])).toBe(0);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM chat_sessions WHERE id = $1", [chatId])).toBe(0);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM tenants WHERE id = $1", [tenantId])).toBe(0);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM loom.timers WHERE execution_id = $1", [exec])).toBe(0);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM loom.leases WHERE execution_id = $1", [exec])).toBe(0);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM loom.timers WHERE execution_id = $1", [other])).toBe(1); // another user's stay
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM loom.leases WHERE execution_id = $1", [other])).toBe(1);
  });

  it("rows: keeps the tenant while a colleague remains, and the colleague's data", async () => {
    const { tenantId, users } = await tenantWith("keep1@work.test", "keep2@work.test");
    const [gone, stays] = users as [(typeof users)[0], (typeof users)[0]];
    const chatId = randomUUID();
    await db.owner.query("INSERT INTO chat_sessions (id, tenant_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [chatId, tenantId, stays.userId]);
    await sql.rows(request(tenantId, gone.userId));
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM tenants WHERE id = $1", [tenantId])).toBe(1);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM users WHERE id = $1", [stays.userId])).toBe(1);
    expect(await count("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM chat_sessions WHERE id = $1", [chatId])).toBe(1);
  });

  it("blobs: removes the user's directory and nothing of the colleague's; twice is fine", async () => {
    const tenantId = randomUUID();
    const [gone, stays] = [randomUUID(), randomUUID()];
    for (const u of [gone, stays]) {
      mkdirSync(join(root, tenantId, u), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(join(root, tenantId, u, "ab"), "x");
    }
    await sql.blobs(request(tenantId, gone));
    await sql.blobs(request(tenantId, gone));
    expect(existsSync(join(root, tenantId, gone))).toBe(false);
    expect(existsSync(join(root, tenantId, stays, "ab"))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("lock: one holder per user at a time, released on request, and per user", async () => {
    const lock = pgErasureLock(app);
    const user = randomUUID();
    const first = await lock(user);
    expect(first).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await lock(user)).toBeNull(); // held (another session)
    const second = await lock(randomUUID());
    expect(second).not.toBeNull();
    await first?.();
    const again = await lock(user);
    expect(again).not.toBeNull();
    await again?.();
    await second?.();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7 (169b): Write the Postgres steps** (`erasure/pgSteps.ts`). `rows` never reads the email (the request transaction already replaced it, and the route cleared the throttle counters). The Loom pool's `search_path` is `loom`, but the statements are schema-qualified anyway.

```ts
import { eraseUserBlobs } from "@docmind/blob-store";
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import { withTenant } from "../db/tenant";
import type { ErasureDeps } from "./eraser";
import type { ErasureRequest } from "./types";

export interface PgErasureOptions {
  /** The docmind_app pool: every statement on it runs under the request's tenant. */
  app: Pool;
  /** The docmind_loom pool (owns the `loom` schema). */
  loom: Pool;
  blobRoot: string;
}

/**
 * A session advisory lock per user (`erasure:<user>`) on a connection of its own, held until released.
 * A dead connection drops the lock by itself. Null when another session holds it.
 */
export function pgErasureLock(pool: Pool): ErasureDeps["lock"] {
  return async (userId) => {
    const key = `erasure:${userId}`;
    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
      const { rows } = await client.query(
        "SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtextextended($1, 0)) AS held",
        [key],
      );
      if (!rows[0].held) {
        client.release();
        return null;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      client.release(err instanceof Error ? err : new Error("lock failed"));
      throw err;
    }
    return async () => {
      try {
        await client.query("SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtextextended($1, 0))", [key]);
        client.release();
      } catch (err) {
        client.release(err instanceof Error ? err : new Error("unlock failed")); // destroys the connection, and the lock with it
      }
    };
  };
}

export function pgErasureSql(o: PgErasureOptions): Pick<ErasureDeps, "lock" | "work" | "rows" | "blobs"> {
  const { app, loom } = o;
  return {
    lock: pgErasureLock(app),

    async work(req) {
      return withTenant(app, req.tenantId, async (tx) => {
        const docs = await tx.query(
          "SELECT id, ingest_generation FROM documents WHERE owner_id = $1",
          [req.userId],
        );
        const chats = await tx.query("SELECT id FROM chat_sessions WHERE user_id = $1", [req.userId]);
        return {
          documents: docs.rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id as string, generation: Number(r.ingest_generation) })),
          chatIds: chats.rows.map((r) => r.id as string),
        };
      });
    },

    async rows(req: ErasureRequest) {
      // The throttle counters are not touched here: they are keyed by a hash of the email, which the request
      // transaction already replaced, so the route clears them (Task 170a).
      // 1. The user row: the foreign keys cascade to sessions, documents (chunks, parsed text, links, entities)
      //    and chats (turn items, reasoning texts).
      await withTenant(app, req.tenantId, (tx) =>
        tx.query("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $1 AND tenant_id = $2", [req.userId, req.tenantId]),
      );
      // 2. Loom retires the timers of terminal executions but has no prefix API: remove what is left.
      //    An execution id of the user always starts with `u_<uuid>` (subject.ts).
      const prefix = `u_${req.userId}`;
      await loom.query("DELETE FROM loom.timers WHERE starts_with(execution_id, $1)", [prefix]);
      await loom.query("DELETE FROM loom.leases WHERE starts_with(execution_id, $1)", [prefix]);
      // 3. The personal tenant goes with its last user (D6). A tenant with other users stays.
      await app.query("SELECT public.docmind_delete_empty_tenant($1)", [req.tenantId]);
    },

    blobs: (req) => eraseUserBlobs(o.blobRoot, req.tenantId, req.userId),
  };
}
```

```ts
import { ConversationNotFoundError } from "@loom/conversation";
import { InboxClosedError } from "@loom/workflow-runtime";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { chatConversationId } from "../chats/ids";
import { executionIdFor } from "../subject";
import { erasureDeps } from "./wire";

const USER = "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111";
const TENANT = "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222";
const CHAT = "44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444";
const who = { tenantId: TENANT, userId: USER };

function parts(over: Partial<Parameters<typeof erasureDeps>[0]> = {}) {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const base: Parameters<typeof erasureDeps>[0] = {
    app: { eraseSubject: async () => ({ outcome: "erased" as const }) },
    runtime: {
      cancel: vi.fn(async (id: string) => {
        calls.push(`cancel:${id}`);
        return true;
      }),
    },
    keys: { destroy: async () => {}, wasDestroyed: async () => true },
    store: { pending: async () => [], mark: async () => {}, retried: async () => {}, get: async () => null },
    sql: { lock: async () => null, work: async () => ({ documents: [], chatIds: [] }), rows: async () => {}, blobs: async () => {} },
    starts: { settle: async () => {} },
    ingest: { cancel: async () => {} },
    chat: {
      stops: { stop: (id: string) => (calls.push(`stop:${id}`), true) },
      conversations: {
        close: vi.fn(async (id: string) => {
          calls.push(`close:${id}`);
          return {} as never;
        }),
      },
    },
    status: { forgetUser: async () => 0 },
    activity: { forgetUser: async () => 0 },
    closeStreams: () => {},
  };
  return { calls, parts: { ...base, ...over } };
}

describe("erasureDeps", () => {
  it("stops a chat by stop signal, then close, and cancels its title execution", async () => {
    const { calls, parts: p } = parts();
    const deps = erasureDeps(p);
    await deps.stopChat(who, CHAT);
    await deps.cancelTitle(who, CHAT);
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      `stop:${chatConversationId(USER, CHAT)}`,
      `close:${chatConversationId(USER, CHAT)}`,
      `cancel:${executionIdFor(USER, `chat-title-${CHAT}`)}`,
    ]);
  });

  it("tolerates a conversation that never started or is closed, and a title that never started", async () => {
    const { parts: p } = parts({
      chat: {
        stops: { stop: () => false },
        conversations: {
          close: async (id: string) => {
            throw id.endsWith("a") ? new ConversationNotFoundError(id) : new InboxClosedError(id, "completed");
          },
        },
      },
      runtime: {
        cancel: async (id: string) => {
          throw new Error(`No execution found for id: ${id}`);
        },
      },
    });
    const deps = erasureDeps(p);
    await expect(deps.stopChat(who, CHAT)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await expect(deps.cancelTitle(who, CHAT)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("rethrows any other failure so the step is retried", async () => {
    const { parts: p } = parts({
      runtime: {
        cancel: async () => {
          throw new RangeError("db down");
        },
      },
    });
    await expect(erasureDeps(p).cancelTitle(who, CHAT)).rejects.toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("without chat, stopping a chat is a no-op", async () => {
    const { parts: p } = parts();
    const { chat: _chat, ...withoutChat } = p;
    await expect(erasureDeps(withoutChat).stopChat(who, CHAT)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("passes the shred the user and no tenant, and asks for parked owners to be shredded", async () => {
    const eraseSubject = vi.fn(async () => ({ outcome: "erased" as const }));
    const { parts: p } = parts({ app: { eraseSubject } });
    await erasureDeps(p).eraseSubject(USER);
    expect(eraseSubject).toHaveBeenCalledWith(USER, undefined, { parked: "shred" });
  });

  it("reads a key store without wasDestroyed as 'not destroyed'", async () => {
    const { parts: p } = parts({ keys: { destroy: async () => {} } });
    expect(await erasureDeps(p).keys.wasDestroyed(USER)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("bounds the wait for a document's pending start", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    try {
      const { parts: p } = parts({ starts: { settle: () => new Promise<void>(() => {}) } });
      const settled = erasureDeps(p).settleStart(who, "d");
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10_000);
      await expect(settled).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers();
    }
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 10 (169b): Write the wiring** (`erasure/wire.ts`).

```ts
import type { Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import { ConversationNotFoundError } from "@loom/conversation";
import type { KeyStore } from "@loom/plugin-crypto";
import { InboxClosedError, type WorkflowRuntime } from "@loom/workflow-runtime";
import { principalOf } from "../chats/access";
import { chatConversationId } from "../chats/ids";
import type { ChatWiring } from "../chats/wiring";
import type { IngestPort } from "../documents/ingest";
import type { StartScheduler } from "../documents/startScheduler";
import { drainWithin } from "../shutdown";
import { executionIdFor } from "../subject";
import type { ErasureDeps } from "./eraser";
import type { ErasureStore } from "./types";

/** The cancel reason lands in the log in the clear: a fixed word, no content. */
const ERASED = "erased";
/** How long the stop step waits for one document's pending ingest start. */
const SETTLE_MS = 5_000;

export interface ErasureParts {
  app: {
    eraseSubject(
      userId: string,
      tenantId: undefined,
      options: { parked: "shred" },
    ): Promise<{ outcome: "erased" | "parked" | "unknown" }>;
  };
  runtime: Pick<WorkflowRuntime, "cancel">;
  keys: Pick<KeyStore, "destroy"> & { wasDestroyed?(subjectId: string): Promise<boolean> };
  store: ErasureDeps["store"];
  sql: Pick<ErasureDeps, "lock" | "work" | "rows" | "blobs">;
  starts: Pick<StartScheduler, "settle">;
  titles?: { idle(): Promise<void> };
  ingest: Pick<IngestPort, "cancel">;
  /** Absent when chat is not composed: there are no conversations to stop. */
  chat?: { stops: Pick<ChatWiring["stops"], "stop">; conversations: Pick<ChatWiring["conversations"], "close"> };
  status: { forgetUser(userId: string): Promise<unknown> };
  activity: { forgetUser(userId: string): Promise<unknown> };
  closeStreams(userId: string): void;
  sleep?: ErasureDeps["sleep"];
}

/** Loom throws a plain Error for an execution that never existed; match its exact message for this id and nothing else. */
const isNoExecution = (err: unknown, executionId: string) =>
  err instanceof Error && err.message === `No execution found for id: ${executionId}`;

/** The production ports of the `Eraser`. */
export function erasureDeps(p: ErasureParts): ErasureDeps {
  return {
    store: p.store,
    ...p.sql,
    closeStreams: p.closeStreams,
    settleStart: (identity, documentId) =>
      drainWithin(p.starts.settle(identity, documentId), SETTLE_MS),
    settleTitles: async () => void (await p.titles?.idle()),
    cancelIngest: (identity, documentId, generation) => p.ingest.cancel(identity, documentId, generation),
    async stopChat(identity: Identity, chatId: string) {
      if (!p.chat) return;
      const conversationId = chatConversationId(identity.userId, chatId);
      p.chat.stops.stop(conversationId); // aborts a running turn at its next checkpoint
      try {
        await p.chat.conversations.close(conversationId, { principal: principalOf(identity) });
      } catch (err) {
        // Never started, or closed already: nothing to wind down.
        if (!(err instanceof ConversationNotFoundError || err instanceof InboxClosedError)) throw err;
      }
    },
    async cancelTitle(identity, chatId) {
      const executionId = executionIdFor(identity.userId, `chat-title-${chatId}`);
      try {
        await p.runtime.cancel(executionId, ERASED, { principal: principalOf(identity) });
      } catch (err) {
        if (!isNoExecution(err, executionId)) throw err;
      }
    },
    eraseSubject: (userId) => p.app.eraseSubject(userId, undefined, { parked: "shred" }),
    keys: {
      destroy: (subjectId) => p.keys.destroy(subjectId),
      wasDestroyed: async (subjectId) => (await p.keys.wasDestroyed?.(subjectId)) === true,
    },
    forgetStatus: (userId) => p.status.forgetUser(userId),
    forgetActivity: (userId) => p.activity.forgetUser(userId),
    ...(p.sleep && { sleep: p.sleep }),
  };
}
```

### Task 170: `DELETE /api/me` with the receipt and the email scrub (170a), `GET /api/erasure/:receipt` (170b) (spec §9 item 34, D1)

Implements D1, D1a and D1b. Two parts, two PRs:

- **170a: `DELETE /api/me`.** The route authenticates, checks the password with the login hasher under the login throttle, and then, in **one transaction**, inserts the `erasure_requests` row (with the sha256 of a fresh receipt token), deletes every session of the user, sets `users.erasing_at` and **replaces `users.email` with `'erased:' || user_id`**. It clears the cookie, answers **202** `{ erasure: "accepted", receipt: <token> }` and starts the eraser in the background (Task 169). From that moment the account is dead: no session exists, login cannot match the old email (and `erasing_at` refuses it a second time), the old email is in no table, and **signing up with the same email succeeds at once** as a new account, even though the old erasure is still pending.
- **170b: `GET /api/erasure/:receipt`.** The unauthenticated, throttled, `no-store` route that turns a receipt into `{state}`.

The route does not wait for the erasure: a running chat turn can hold the shred for as long as the turn runs (open question 2), and a crash must not lose the request (it is durable, and boot resumes it).

The receipt is the caller's only handle once the session is gone. It is 32 random bytes from `node:crypto` (base64url, 43 characters); the database holds only `sha256(token text)`; the token is never stored and never logged (Task 168b's `newReceipt`/`hashReceipt`).

The `rows` step can no longer read the email to clear the throttle counters (they are keyed by a hash of it), so **the route clears the login and sign-up counters of the email before it records the request**.

**Files (170a, 10):**
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/auth.ts` (`DeleteAccountRequest`, `ErasureReceipt`, `DeleteAccountResponse`), `packages/contracts/src/auth.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/auth/src/authService.ts` (`confirmPassword`)
- Create: `packages/auth/src/confirmPassword.test.ts`
- Create: `apps/server/app/erasure/account.ts` (`AccountErasure`, `pgAccountErasure`)
- Modify: `apps/server/app/auth/routes.ts` (the route), `apps/server/app/compose.ts` (builds the `AccountErasure`, passes it to `authRoutes`; one `PgErasureStore` shared with the eraser)
- Test: `apps/server/app/auth/deleteMe.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/erasure/account.pg.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/auth/erasing.pg.test.ts`

**Files (170b, 8):**
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/auth.ts` (`ErasureStatusResponse`), `packages/contracts/src/auth.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/auth/src/throttle.ts` (`Scope` gains `"erasure"`, `POLICIES.erasureStatusIp`), `packages/auth/src/throttle.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/server/app/erasure/account.ts` (`AccountErasure.status`), `apps/server/app/auth/routes.ts` (the route)
- Test: `apps/server/app/auth/erasureStatus.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/erasure/account.pg.test.ts` (one case), `apps/server/app/auth/deleteMe.test.ts` (the fake gains `status`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 168 (`PgErasureStore.insert(tx, userId, tenantId, receiptHash)`, `status(receiptHash)`, `newReceipt`, `hashReceipt`, `users.erasing_at`, the app role's `UPDATE` on `users`); Task 169 (`Eraser.run`, `buildServer` returning `eraser`); existing `requireAuth`, `readJson`, `json`, `sendError`, `Throttle`, `POLICIES`, `accountKey`, `ipKey`, `clientIp`, `clearedSessionCookie`, `AuthService`, `verifyPassword`, `verifyAgainstDecoy`, `withTenant`.
- Produces:
  - Contract `DeleteAccountRequest { password: string }` (1 to 128 characters, like login), `ErasureReceipt` (base64url, exactly 43 characters) and `DeleteAccountResponse { erasure: "accepted", receipt: ErasureReceipt }`; in 170b `ErasureStatusResponse { state: "pending" | "done" }`.
  - `AuthService.confirmPassword(identity, email, password): Promise<boolean>`. True only when the password is right **and** the account it belongs to is `identity`; the same work (one hash check, or the decoy) for every outcome.
  - `AccountErasure { request(identity, receiptHash): Promise<void>; start(userId): void }` (170b adds `status(receiptHash)`) and `pgAccountErasure({ pool, store, eraser })`. `request` is the one transaction and is idempotent (a second call changes nothing: the first receipt stays, the scrub repeats harmlessly). `start` is fire-and-forget.
  - `AuthRoutesDeps.erasure?: AccountErasure`. Without it neither erasure route is registered.
  - Answers: 202 `{ erasure: "accepted", receipt }` with the session cookie cleared and `Cache-Control: no-store`; 400 `invalid_request`; 401 `unauthorized` (no session); 401 `invalid_credentials` (wrong password; the session stays valid); 429 `too_many_requests` with `Retry-After`; 403 from the origin guard for a cross-origin write.
  - Login: after the 202 the old email matches no account, so login answers 401 `invalid_credentials` after the decoy hash; if `erasing_at` were set on a row that still carries its email, `auth_find_login` refuses it with the same answer (second safeguard). Sign-up: with the old email it creates a **new** user and tenant and answers the usual 202; there is no special case.

#### 170a: `DELETE /api/me`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing contract test.** Append to `packages/contracts/src/auth.test.ts` (and add `DeleteAccountRequest`, `DeleteAccountResponse` and `ErasureReceipt` to its import):

```ts
describe("delete account contract", () => {
  it("needs a password of 1 to 128 characters and ignores nothing else it is given", () => {
    expect(DeleteAccountRequest.parse({ password: "x" })).toEqual({ password: "x" });
    expect(DeleteAccountRequest.safeParse({}).success).toBe(false);
    expect(DeleteAccountRequest.safeParse({ password: "" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(DeleteAccountRequest.safeParse({ password: "p".repeat(129) }).success).toBe(false);
    // a short password is fine here: it only has to match the one on file (no minimum, like login)
    expect(DeleteAccountRequest.safeParse({ password: "short" }).success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("answers with { erasure: 'accepted', receipt } and the receipt is 43 base64url characters", () => {
    const receipt = "A".repeat(43);
    expect(DeleteAccountResponse.parse({ erasure: "accepted", receipt })).toEqual({ erasure: "accepted", receipt });
    expect(DeleteAccountResponse.safeParse({ erasure: "done", receipt }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(DeleteAccountResponse.safeParse({ erasure: "accepted" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(ErasureReceipt.safeParse("a-b_c".padEnd(43, "0")).success).toBe(true);
    for (const bad of ["", "A".repeat(42), "A".repeat(44), `${"A".repeat(42)}=`, `${"A".repeat(42)}+`, `${"A".repeat(42)}/`, `${"A".repeat(42)} `])
      expect(ErasureReceipt.safeParse(bad).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail.** `pnpm -C packages/contracts vitest run src/auth.test.ts`. Expected: FAIL (`DeleteAccountRequest` is not exported).

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the contract** to `packages/contracts/src/auth.ts`:

```ts
/** `DELETE /api/me`: the account's own password, so a stolen session alone cannot erase it. No minimum, like login. */
export const DeleteAccountRequest = z.object({ password: z.string().min(1).max(128) });
export type DeleteAccountRequest = z.infer<typeof DeleteAccountRequest>;

/** An erasure receipt: 32 random bytes as base64url, which is 43 characters (no padding). The caller's only handle once the session is gone. */
export const ErasureReceipt = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
export type ErasureReceipt = z.infer<typeof ErasureReceipt>;

/**
 * The only success answer (202). The erasure runs in the background: it can wait for a chat turn that is
 * still running, and it resumes after a restart. `receipt` is shown once; `GET /api/erasure/:receipt` reads it.
 */
export const DeleteAccountResponse = z.object({ erasure: z.literal("accepted"), receipt: ErasureReceipt });
export type DeleteAccountResponse = z.infer<typeof DeleteAccountResponse>;
```

Run the Step 2 command. Expected: PASS. Then build the package (`apps/server` imports `@docmind/contracts` from `dist`): `pnpm -C packages/contracts build`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the failing `confirmPassword` test** (`packages/auth/src/confirmPassword.test.ts`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { AuthService } from "./authService";
import { MemoryAuthStore } from "./memoryStore";
import * as password from "./password";

const EMAIL = "ada@example.com";
const PASSWORD = "correct horse battery";

async function setup() {
  const store = new MemoryAuthStore();
  const service = new AuthService(store);
  await service.signUp(EMAIL, PASSWORD);
  const login = await store.findLogin(EMAIL);
  if (!login) throw new Error("sign-up did not create the account");
  const identity = { userId: login.userId, tenantId: login.tenantId };
  return { service, identity };
}

describe("AuthService.confirmPassword", () => {
  it("is true for the account's own password", async () => {
    const { service, identity } = await setup();
    expect(await service.confirmPassword(identity, EMAIL, PASSWORD)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("is false for a wrong password", async () => {
    const { service, identity } = await setup();
    expect(await service.confirmPassword(identity, EMAIL, "not the password")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("is false when the right password belongs to a different account than the identity", async () => {
    const { service, identity } = await setup();
    await service.signUp("bob@example.com", PASSWORD);
    expect(await service.confirmPassword(identity, "bob@example.com", PASSWORD)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("is false for an unknown email, after the same hash work", async () => {
    const { service, identity } = await setup();
    const decoy = vi.spyOn(password, "verifyAgainstDecoy");
    expect(await service.confirmPassword(identity, "nobody@example.com", PASSWORD)).toBe(false);
    expect(decoy).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    decoy.mockRestore();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `confirmPassword`** in `packages/auth/src/authService.ts` (after `logIn`):

```ts
  /**
   * True when `password` is the password of the account `email` AND that account is `identity`: the check
   * behind actions that need the password again (account erasure). It does the same work for every
   * outcome (a hash check, or the decoy for an unknown email) and never throws on a bad hash.
   */
  async confirmPassword(identity: Identity, email: string, password: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const login = await this.store.findLogin(email);
    if (!login) {
      await verifyAgainstDecoy(password);
      return false;
    }
    const matches = await verifyPassword(login.passwordHash, password);
    return matches && login.userId === identity.userId && login.tenantId === identity.tenantId;
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing route tests** (`apps/server/app/auth/deleteMe.test.ts`). The store, throttle and eraser are in memory; the server is the real one, so the origin guard and the body reader are in the path.

```ts
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import { AuthService, POLICIES, PasswordAuthenticator, SESSION_COOKIE, accountKey } from "@docmind/auth";
import { MemoryAuthStore, MemoryThrottle } from "@docmind/auth/testing";
import { DeleteAccountResponse, ErrorBody } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { AccountErasure } from "../erasure/account";
import { hashReceipt } from "../erasure/receipt";
import { createHttpServer } from "../server";
import { authRoutes } from "./routes";

const EMAIL = "ada@example.com";
const PASSWORD = "correct horse battery";

const closers: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];
afterEach(async () => {
  await Promise.all(closers.splice(0).map((close) => close()));
});

async function start(erasure: AccountErasure | undefined) {
  const store = new MemoryAuthStore();
  const throttle = new MemoryThrottle();
  throttle.now = () => 1_000_000;
  const service = new AuthService(store);
  const server = createHttpServer(
    authRoutes({
      service,
      authenticator: new PasswordAuthenticator(store),
      store,
      throttle,
      secureCookies: false,
      trustProxy: false,
      ...(erasure && { erasure }),
    }),
  );
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
  closers.push(
    () =>
      new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        server.close(() => resolve());
        server.closeAllConnections();
      }),
  );
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
  await service.signUp(EMAIL, PASSWORD);
  const started = await service.logIn(EMAIL, PASSWORD);
  if (!started) throw new Error("login failed");
  const cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${started.token}`;
  const del = (body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}, withCookie = true) =>
    fetch(`${base}/api/me`, {
      method: "DELETE",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...(withCookie && { cookie }), ...headers },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  const me = () => fetch(`${base}/api/me`, { headers: { cookie } });
  return { base, service, store, throttle, started, del, me, logIn: (p: string) => service.logIn(EMAIL, p) };
}

function fakeErasure() {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const erasure: AccountErasure = {
    request: vi.fn(async (_identity, _receiptHash) => void calls.push("request")),
    start: vi.fn(() => void calls.push("start")),
  };
  return { erasure, calls };
}

describe("DELETE /api/me", () => {
  it("is not a route without an erasure wiring", async () => {
    const api = await start(undefined);
    expect((await api.del({ password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("needs a session", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const res = await api.del({ password: PASSWORD }, {}, false);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(ErrorBody.parse(await res.json()).error.code).toBe("unauthorized");
    expect(erasure.request).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("refuses a body without a password", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    expect((await api.del({})).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await api.del({ password: "" })).status).toBe(400);
    expect(erasure.request).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("refuses a cross-origin write before it reads anything", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const res = await api.del({ password: PASSWORD }, { origin: "https://evil.example" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect(erasure.request).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a wrong password is 401 invalid_credentials, erases nothing, and leaves the session valid", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const res = await api.del({ password: "not the password" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(ErrorBody.parse(await res.json()).error.code).toBe("invalid_credentials");
    expect(res.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();
    expect(erasure.request).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(erasure.start).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect((await api.me()).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("the right password: requests the erasure, then starts it, answers 202 and clears the cookie", async () => {
    const { erasure, calls } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const res = await api.del({ password: PASSWORD });
    expect(res.status).toBe(202);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    const body = DeleteAccountResponse.parse(await res.json());
    expect(body.erasure).toBe("accepted");
    expect(body.receipt).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
    const cookie = res.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "";
    expect(cookie).toContain(`${SESSION_COOKIE}=;`);
    expect(cookie).toContain("Max-Age=0");
    expect(calls).toEqual(["request", "start"]);
    // The request gets the HASH of the receipt, never the token.
    expect(erasure.request).toHaveBeenCalledWith(api.started.identity, hashReceipt(body.receipt));
    expect(erasure.start).toHaveBeenCalledWith(api.started.identity.userId);
  });

  it("gives every erasure a fresh receipt, and neither stores nor logs the token", async () => {
    const out = ["log", "error", "warn", "info"].map((m) => vi.spyOn(console, m as "log").mockImplementation(() => {}));
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const first = DeleteAccountResponse.parse(await (await api.del({ password: PASSWORD })).json());
    const second = DeleteAccountResponse.parse(await (await api.del({ password: PASSWORD })).json()); // the fake keeps the session
    expect(first.receipt).not.toBe(second.receipt);
    for (const call of vi.mocked(erasure.request).mock.calls) {
      const hash = call[1] as Buffer;
      expect(hash).toHaveLength(32);
      expect(hash.toString("base64url")).not.toBe(first.receipt);
    }
    expect(JSON.stringify(out.flatMap((s) => s.mock.calls))).not.toContain(first.receipt);
    for (const s of out) s.mockRestore();
  });

  it("clears the email's login and sign-up counters BEFORE it records the request (the email is replaced in that transaction)", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const signUpKey = accountKey("signup", EMAIL);
    for (let i = 0; i < POLICIES.signUpAccount.free + 2; i++) await api.throttle.fail(signUpKey, POLICIES.signUpAccount);
    expect(await api.throttle.retryAfter([signUpKey])).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    let lockedAtRequest = -1;
    vi.mocked(erasure.request).mockImplementation(async () => {
      lockedAtRequest = await api.throttle.retryAfter([signUpKey]);
    });
    expect((await api.del({ password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(202);
    expect(lockedAtRequest).toBe(0); // already cleared when the request was made
    expect(await api.throttle.retryAfter([signUpKey])).toBe(0);
  });

  it("starts nothing when the request cannot be recorded, and does not claim success", async () => {
    const errors = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    erasure.request = vi.fn(async () => {
      throw new RangeError("db down");
    });
    const api = await start(erasure);
    const res = await api.del({ password: PASSWORD });
    expect(res.status).toBe(500);
    expect(res.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();
    expect(erasure.start).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect((await api.me()).status).toBe(200); // still signed in: nothing happened
    errors.mockRestore();
  });

  it("is throttled like login: guesses share the account counter, and a locked caller is not even checked", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    for (let i = 0; i < POLICIES.loginAccount.free; i++)
      expect((await api.del({ password: "guess" })).status).toBe(401);
    const locked = await api.del({ password: "guess" });
    expect(locked.status).toBe(429);
    expect(Number(locked.headers.get("retry-after"))).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    // even the right password waits out the lock, and the lock covers login too (one counter)
    expect((await api.del({ password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(429);
    expect(erasure.request).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const login = await fetch(`${api.base}/api/auth/login`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email: EMAIL, password: PASSWORD }),
    });
    expect(login.status).toBe(429);
  });

  it("a right password clears the account counter", async () => {
    const { erasure } = fakeErasure();
    const api = await start(erasure);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await api.del({ password: "guess" });
    expect((await api.del({ password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(202);
    for (let i = 0; i < POLICIES.loginAccount.free; i++)
      expect((await api.del({ password: "guess" })).status).toBe(401); // a fresh budget of free guesses
  });
});
```

  The loop after the 202 is the check: 5 more wrong guesses are free again because the success cleared the counter.

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the account erasure** (`erasure/account.ts`). The transaction runs under the user's tenant, so row-level security applies to the session delete and the `users` update.

```ts
import type { Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import { withTenant } from "../db/tenant";
import type { Eraser } from "./eraser";
import type { ErasureStore } from "./types";

/** What `DELETE /api/me` needs from erasure: record it durably, then start it. */
export interface AccountErasure {
  /**
   * One transaction: the durable request (with `receiptHash`, the sha256 of the receipt token), every session of
   * the user gone, `erasing_at` set, and the email replaced by `erased:<user id>`. Idempotent: a second call (a
   * retried request) changes nothing that matters: the first receipt stays and the scrub repeats harmlessly.
   */
  request(identity: Identity, receiptHash: Buffer): Promise<void>;
  /** Runs the erasure in the background. Never throws and never waits. */
  start(userId: string): void;
}

export function pgAccountErasure(o: {
  pool: Pool;
  store: Pick<ErasureStore, "insert">;
  eraser: Pick<Eraser, "run">;
}): AccountErasure {
  return {
    async request(identity, receiptHash) {
      await withTenant(o.pool, identity.tenantId, async (tx) => {
        await o.store.insert(tx, identity.userId, identity.tenantId, receiptHash);
        await tx.query("DELETE FROM auth_sessions WHERE user_id = $1", [identity.userId]);
        // The address is free from here: login can no longer match it (`erasing_at` refuses the row a second time),
        // sign-up with it makes a new account, and no table still holds it. The app role may update `users`
        // under its own tenant's policy (0002, pinned by erasureRequests.pg.test.ts). `'erased:' || id` is unique
        // and not a valid email, so nobody can sign up or log in as it; the `rows` step deletes the row.
        await tx.query(
          "UPDATE users SET erasing_at = COALESCE(erasing_at, now()), email = 'erased:' || id::text WHERE id = $1",
          [identity.userId],
        );
      });
    },
    start(userId) {
      void o.eraser.run(userId); // run() never rejects
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Add the route** to `auth/routes.ts`. Imports gain `DeleteAccountRequest`, `type DeleteAccountResponse`, `type AccountErasure` and `newReceipt` (from `../erasure/receipt`); `AuthRoutesDeps` gains `erasure?: AccountErasure` (doc: "Absent: `DELETE /api/me` is not a route"); the returned object gains, after `GET /api/me`:

```ts
    // The password again (a stolen session alone must not erase an account), under the login throttle: the
    // same account and address counters, so guesses here and at login draw from one budget. The request, the
    // session wipe, `erasing_at` and the email scrub are one transaction; the erasure itself runs in the
    // background. The answer carries the receipt (shown once; only its hash is stored).
    ...(erasure && {
      "DELETE /api/me": requireAuth(authenticator, async (req, res, identity) => {
        const body = await readJson(req, res, DeleteAccountRequest);
        if (!body) return;
        const email = await store.findEmail(identity);
        if (!email) return sendError(res, 401, "unauthorized", "authentication required");
        const ip = ipKey("login", clientIp(req, trustProxy));
        const account = accountKey("login", email);
        await throttle.fail(account, POLICIES.loginAccount);
        const wait = await throttle.retryAfter([ip, account]);
        if (wait > 0) return tooMany(res, wait);
        if (!(await service.confirmPassword(identity, email, body.password))) {
          await throttle.fail(ip, POLICIES.loginIp);
          return sendError(res, 401, "invalid_credentials", "password is incorrect");
        }
        await throttle.clear(account);
        // The counters are keyed by a hash of the email, which the request below replaces: clear the sign-up
        // counter now, while the email is known (the eraser's `rows` step cannot read it any more).
        await throttle.clear(accountKey("signup", email));
        const receipt = newReceipt();
        await erasure.request(identity, receipt.hash);
        res.setHeader("set-cookie", clearedSessionCookie(secureCookies));
        res.setHeader("cache-control", "no-store");
        json(res, 202, { erasure: "accepted", receipt: receipt.token } satisfies DeleteAccountResponse);
        erasure.start(identity.userId);
      }),
    }),
```

  with `const { service, authenticator, store, throttle, secureCookies, trustProxy, erasure } = deps;`.

- [ ] **Step 12: Write the failing Postgres tests.** First `erasure/account.pg.test.ts`: the transaction, on the real tables.

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { newSessionToken } from "@docmind/auth";
import { Pool } from "pg";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { PgAuthStore } from "../auth/pgAuthStore";
import { migrate, setAppRolePassword } from "../db/migrate";
import { APP_PASSWORD, PG_URL, type TestDb, createTestDb } from "../db/testSupport";
import { pgAccountErasure } from "./account";
import { PgErasureStore } from "./pgStore";
import { newReceipt } from "./receipt";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("the account erasure transaction (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let app: Pool;
  let auth: PgAuthStore;
  const run = vi.fn(async () => {});

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    await migrate(db.owner);
    await setAppRolePassword(db.owner, APP_PASSWORD);
    app = new Pool({ connectionString: db.appUrl });
    auth = new PgAuthStore(app);
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await app?.end();
    await db?.drop();
  });

  async function account(email: string) {
    const identity = { tenantId: randomUUID(), userId: randomUUID() };
    expect(await auth.createAccount({ ...identity, email, passwordHash: "x" })).toBe(true);
    const sessions: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      const { tokenHash } = newSessionToken();
      await auth.createSession({ ...identity, tokenHash, expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000) });
      sessions.push(tokenHash);
    }
    return { identity, sessions };
  }
  const erasure = () => pgAccountErasure({ pool: app, store: new PgErasureStore(app), eraser: { run } });

  it("records the request, deletes every session of the user and only theirs, marks the user, and frees the email", async () => {
    const ada = await account("ada@erase.test");
    const bob = await account("bob@erase.test");
    const receipt = newReceipt();
    await erasure().request(ada.identity, receipt.hash);
    expect(await new PgErasureStore(app).status(receipt.hash)).toBe("pending");
    const stored = await db.owner.query("SELECT receipt_hash FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [ada.identity.userId]);
    expect(stored.rows[0].receipt_hash).toEqual(receipt.hash); // the hash, not the token
    expect(stored.rows[0].receipt_hash.toString("base64url")).not.toBe(receipt.token);
    // the email: replaced in the same transaction, the neighbour's untouched, the old address in no row
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [ada.identity.userId])).rows[0].email).toBe(`erased:${ada.identity.userId}`);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [bob.identity.userId])).rows[0].email).toBe("bob@erase.test");
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM users WHERE email = 'ada@erase.test'")).rows[0].n).toBe(0);

    const req = await db.owner.query("SELECT tenant_id, state, steps FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [ada.identity.userId]);
    expect(req.rows).toEqual([{ tenant_id: ada.identity.tenantId, state: "pending", steps: [] }]);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM auth_sessions WHERE user_id = $1", [ada.identity.userId])).rows[0].n).toBe(0);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM auth_sessions WHERE user_id = $1", [bob.identity.userId])).rows[0].n).toBe(2);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT erasing_at IS NOT NULL AS marked FROM users WHERE id = $1", [ada.identity.userId])).rows[0].marked).toBe(true);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT erasing_at IS NULL AS clean FROM users WHERE id = $1", [bob.identity.userId])).rows[0].clean).toBe(true);
    for (const hash of ada.sessions) expect(await auth.findSession(hash)).toBeNull();
    expect(await auth.findSession(bob.sessions[0] as string)).toEqual(bob.identity);
  });

  it("is idempotent: a second request changes nothing, and the first receipt is the one that works", async () => {
    const cy = await account("cy@erase.test");
    const [one, two] = [newReceipt(), newReceipt()];
    await erasure().request(cy.identity, one.hash);
    const first = (await db.owner.query("SELECT erasing_at FROM users WHERE id = $1", [cy.identity.userId])).rows[0].erasing_at as Date;
    await erasure().request(cy.identity, two.hash);
    const second = (await db.owner.query("SELECT erasing_at FROM users WHERE id = $1", [cy.identity.userId])).rows[0].erasing_at as Date;
    expect(second.getTime()).toBe(first.getTime());
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM erasure_requests WHERE user_id = $1", [cy.identity.userId])).rows[0].n).toBe(1);
    const store = new PgErasureStore(app);
    expect(await store.status(one.hash)).toBe("pending");
    expect(await store.status(two.hash)).toBeNull();
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [cy.identity.userId])).rows[0].email).toBe(`erased:${cy.identity.userId}`);
  });

  it("is all or nothing: when the insert fails the sessions stay and the email is not scrubbed", async () => {
    const dee = await account("dee@erase.test");
    const broken = pgAccountErasure({
      pool: app,
      store: {
        insert: async () => {
          throw new RangeError("insert failed");
        },
      },
      eraser: { run },
    });
    await expect(broken.request(dee.identity, newReceipt().hash)).rejects.toThrow(RangeError);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [dee.identity.userId])).rows[0].email).toBe("dee@erase.test");
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM auth_sessions WHERE user_id = $1", [dee.identity.userId])).rows[0].n).toBe(2);
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT erasing_at IS NULL AS clean FROM users WHERE id = $1", [dee.identity.userId])).rows[0].clean).toBe(true);
  });

  it("start() runs the eraser for that user and never throws", () => {
    const id = randomUUID();
    erasure().start(id);
    expect(run).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id);
  });
});
```

  Second, `auth/erasing.pg.test.ts`: what login and sign-up do once the request transaction has run. The request is made with the real `pgAccountErasure` (the eraser is a stub: nothing is erased, the account is only requested), so the email is scrubbed exactly as in production.

```ts
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import { AuthService, PasswordAuthenticator } from "@docmind/auth";
import { ErrorBody, SignUpResponse } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { Pool } from "pg";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { migrate, setAppRolePassword } from "../db/migrate";
import { APP_PASSWORD, PG_URL, type TestDb, createTestDb } from "../db/testSupport";
import { pgAccountErasure } from "../erasure/account";
import { PgErasureStore } from "../erasure/pgStore";
import { newReceipt } from "../erasure/receipt";
import { createHttpServer } from "../server";
import { PgAuthStore } from "./pgAuthStore";
import { PgThrottle } from "./pgThrottle";
import { authRoutes } from "./routes";

const PASSWORD = "correct horse battery";
const NEW_PASSWORD = "another long password";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("an account being erased: login is refused, the email is free (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let app: Pool;
  let server: ReturnType<typeof createHttpServer>;
  let base: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    await migrate(db.owner);
    await setAppRolePassword(db.owner, APP_PASSWORD);
    app = new Pool({ connectionString: db.appUrl });
    const store = new PgAuthStore(app);
    server = createHttpServer(
      authRoutes({
        service: new AuthService(store),
        authenticator: new PasswordAuthenticator(store),
        store,
        throttle: new PgThrottle(app),
        secureCookies: false,
        trustProxy: false,
      }),
    );
    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
    base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    if (server) {
      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        server.close(() => resolve());
        server.closeAllConnections();
      });
    }
    await app?.end();
    await db?.drop();
  });

  const post = (path: string, body: unknown) =>
    fetch(`${base}${path}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  const signUp = async (email: string) => expect((await post("/api/auth/sign-up", { email, password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(202);
  const idOf = async (email: string) =>
    (await db.owner.query("SELECT id, tenant_id FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email])).rows[0] as { id: string; tenant_id: string };

  it("after the request: login cannot match the old email, the same email signs up at once as a new account, and a neighbour is unaffected", async () => {
    await signUp("ada@erase.test");
    await signUp("bob@erase.test");
    expect((await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "ada@erase.test", password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(200);
    const ada = await idOf("ada@erase.test");
    const oldHash = (await db.owner.query("SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE id = $1", [ada.id])).rows[0].password_hash;

    await pgAccountErasure({ pool: app, store: new PgErasureStore(app), eraser: { run: async () => {} } }).request(
      { userId: ada.id, tenantId: ada.tenant_id },
      newReceipt().hash,
    );
    expect((await db.owner.query("SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1", [ada.id])).rows[0].email).toBe(`erased:${ada.id}`);

    // Login: no account has that email any more. The answer is the one for a wrong password.
    const refused = await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "ada@erase.test", password: PASSWORD });
    const wrong = await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "bob@erase.test", password: "not the password" });
    expect(refused.status).toBe(401);
    expect(ErrorBody.parse(await refused.json())).toEqual(ErrorBody.parse(await wrong.json()));
    expect(refused.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();

    // Sign-up with the same email succeeds at once, while the old erasure is still pending: a NEW user and tenant.
    const again = await post("/api/auth/sign-up", { email: "ada@erase.test", password: NEW_PASSWORD });
    expect(again.status).toBe(202);
    expect(SignUpResponse.parse(await again.json())).toEqual({ status: "accepted" });
    const fresh = await idOf("ada@erase.test");
    expect(fresh.id).not.toBe(ada.id);
    expect(fresh.tenant_id).not.toBe(ada.tenant_id);
    // The old row is untouched apart from the placeholder (same password hash, still marked), and the new account logs in.
    const old = (await db.owner.query("SELECT password_hash, erasing_at IS NOT NULL AS erasing FROM users WHERE id = $1", [ada.id])).rows[0];
    expect(old).toEqual({ password_hash: oldHash, erasing: true });
    expect((await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "ada@erase.test", password: NEW_PASSWORD })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "ada@erase.test", password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(401); // the old password is dead

    expect((await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "bob@erase.test", password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("erasing_at alone still refuses login (the second safeguard), whatever the email on the row", async () => {
    await signUp("cy@erase.test");
    expect((await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "cy@erase.test", password: PASSWORD })).status).toBe(200);
    await db.owner.query("UPDATE users SET erasing_at = now() WHERE email = 'cy@erase.test'"); // marked, email NOT scrubbed
    const refused = await post("/api/auth/login", { email: "cy@erase.test", password: PASSWORD });
    expect(refused.status).toBe(401);
    expect(refused.headers.get("set-cookie")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import { AuthService, POLICIES, PasswordAuthenticator } from "@docmind/auth";
import { MemoryAuthStore, MemoryThrottle } from "@docmind/auth/testing";
import { ErasureStatusResponse, ErrorBody } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { AccountErasure } from "../erasure/account";
import { hashReceipt, newReceipt } from "../erasure/receipt";
import { createHttpServer } from "../server";
import { authRoutes } from "./routes";

const closers: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];
afterEach(async () => {
  await Promise.all(closers.splice(0).map((close) => close()));
});

async function start(withErasure = true) {
  const store = new MemoryAuthStore();
  const throttle = new MemoryThrottle();
  throttle.now = () => 1_000_000;
  const known = new Map<string, "pending" | "done">(); // receipt hash (hex) -> state
  const asked: Buffer[] = [];
  const erasure: AccountErasure = {
    request: async () => {},
    start: () => {},
    status: vi.fn(async (hash: Buffer) => {
      asked.push(hash);
      return known.get(hash.toString("hex")) ?? null;
    }),
  };
  const server = createHttpServer(
    authRoutes({
      service: new AuthService(store),
      authenticator: new PasswordAuthenticator(store),
      store,
      throttle,
      secureCookies: false,
      trustProxy: false,
      ...(withErasure && { erasure }),
    }),
  );
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
  closers.push(
    () =>
      new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        server.close(() => resolve());
        server.closeAllConnections();
      }),
  );
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
  const get = (receipt: string) => fetch(`${base}/api/erasure/${receipt}`); // no cookie, no origin
  const issue = (state: "pending" | "done") => {
    const r = newReceipt();
    known.set(r.hash.toString("hex"), state);
    return r;
  };
  return { base, get, issue, known, asked, erasure, throttle };
}

describe("GET /api/erasure/:receipt", () => {
  it("is not a route without an erasure wiring", async () => {
    const api = await start(false);
    expect((await api.get(newReceipt().token)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("answers pending, then done, to a caller with no session, and never caches", async () => {
    const api = await start();
    const r = api.issue("pending");
    const pending = await api.get(r.token);
    expect(pending.status).toBe(200);
    expect(pending.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(ErasureStatusResponse.parse(await pending.json())).toEqual({ state: "pending" });
    api.known.set(r.hash.toString("hex"), "done");
    const done = await api.get(r.token);
    expect(done.status).toBe(200);
    expect(done.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(ErasureStatusResponse.parse(await done.json())).toEqual({ state: "done" });
  });

  it("looks the receipt up by its hash, never by the token", async () => {
    const api = await start();
    const r = api.issue("pending");
    await api.get(r.token);
    expect(api.asked).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(api.asked[0]?.equals(hashReceipt(r.token))).toBe(true);
    expect(api.asked[0]).toHaveLength(32);
  });

  it("answers an unknown receipt 404, the same as a malformed one, with no-store", async () => {
    const api = await start();
    const unknown = await api.get(newReceipt().token);
    expect(unknown.status).toBe(404);
    expect(unknown.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    const body = ErrorBody.parse(await unknown.json());
    expect(body.error.code).toBe("not_found");
    for (const bad of ["x", "A".repeat(42), "A".repeat(44), `${"A".repeat(42)}=`, `${"A".repeat(42)}!`, `${"A".repeat(41)}%2F_`]) {
      const res = await api.get(bad);
      expect(res.status).toBe(404);
      expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
      expect(ErrorBody.parse(await res.json())).toEqual(body); // indistinguishable from an unknown receipt
    }
    expect(api.erasure.status).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // the malformed ones never reached the database
  });

  it("is throttled per IP: every request counts, then 429 with Retry-After and no-store", async () => {
    const api = await start();
    const r = api.issue("pending");
    for (let i = 0; i < POLICIES.erasureStatusIp.free; i++) expect((await api.get(r.token)).status).toBe(200);
    const locked = await api.get(r.token);
    expect(locked.status).toBe(429);
    expect(Number(locked.headers.get("retry-after"))).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(locked.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(ErrorBody.parse(await locked.json()).error.code).toBe("too_many_requests");
    expect((await api.get("not-a-receipt")).status).toBe(429); // a malformed token is counted and locked out too
  });

  it("does not share its budget with login or sign-up", async () => {
    const api = await start();
    const r = api.issue("pending");
    for (let i = 0; i < POLICIES.erasureStatusIp.free + 1; i++) await api.get(r.token);
    const login = await fetch(`${api.base}/api/auth/login`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email: "nobody@example.com", password: "x" }),
    });
    expect(login.status).toBe(401); // not 429: the login budget is untouched
  });
});
```

```ts
    // No session: the receipt is the credential (256 random bits), and the answer is a state and nothing else.
    // Every request is counted per client address first (the sign-up pattern: count, then check), so a flood
    // is slowed whether it sends real tokens, unknown ones or garbage. A malformed token never reaches the
    // database, and looks exactly like an unknown one. Nothing about the answer is cacheable.
    "GET /api/erasure/:receipt": async (req, res, { params }) => {
      res.setHeader("cache-control", "no-store");
      const ip = ipKey("erasure", clientIp(req, trustProxy));
      await throttle.fail(ip, POLICIES.erasureStatusIp);
      const wait = await throttle.retryAfter([ip]);
      if (wait > 0) return tooMany(res, wait);
      const receipt = ErasureReceipt.safeParse(params.receipt);
      const state = receipt.success ? await erasure.status(hashReceipt(receipt.data)) : null;
      if (!state) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such erasure");
      json(res, 200, { state } satisfies ErasureStatusResponse);
    },
```

  The route is registered only when `erasure` is wired, like `DELETE /api/me`, so `compose.ts` needs no change.
